### [1] [Exact quantum spin liquids with topological order on maple-leaf and trellis lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04319)
*Li Ern Chern,Roderich Moessner,Claudio Castelnovo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文在五配位的maple-leaf和trellis晶格上构建了具有键依赖各向异性相互作用的自旋模型，得到类似Kitaev蜂窝模型的精确Z₂量子自旋液体，并通过复本交换量子蒙特卡洛模拟等手段表征其通量序、拓扑相图及任意子对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev蜂窝模型展示了精确可解的量子自旋液体，但将其推广到其他晶格（尤其是含奇数长度基本胞的晶格）面临困难，例如简并微扰理论无法处理奇数长度的基本胞元。本文旨在构建和表征更广泛的Kitaev型自旋模型以拓展量子自旋液体的可实现晶格类别。

Method: 在五配位的maple-leaf和trellis晶格上构造含键依赖各向异性相互作用的精确可解自旋模型，利用通量扇区、陈数（Chern number）和拓扑激发对基态进行刻画；采用复本交换量子蒙特卡洛（replica exchange quantum Monte Carlo）模拟确定低温柔序通量构型；推导马约拉纳费米子质量项的解析表达式以表征相变。

Result: 低温柔序下每个单位三角形的通量为±π/2且符号全局一致，六边形（正方形）通量为0（π）；得到涵盖Z₂和Ising拓扑序的相图；在二聚体极限中建立了单个涡旋与两类任意子的一一对应；发现maple-leaf模型两个二聚体极限在无涡旋扇区可平滑连接，但在双涡旋扇区被费米子能隙闭合所分隔。

Conclusion: 该工作表明Kitaev型精确可解量子自旋液体可拓展至五配位晶格，并成功克服了奇数长度胞元在微扰理论中的障碍；两个拓扑相之间的转变可由马约拉纳费米子质量项消失来识别，这为更广泛晶格上量子自旋液体的理论和数值研究提供了框架。

Abstract: We construct spin models with bond-dependent anisotropic interactions on the penta-coordinated maple-leaf and trellis lattices, which yield exact $\mathbb{Z}_2$ quantum spin liquids akin to the Kitaev honeycomb model. We characterize the resulting ground states by their flux sectors, Chern numbers, and topological excitations. Using replica exchange quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we find that the gauge fluxes are ordered at sufficiently low temperatures such that every unit triangle has $\pm π/2$-flux, where the sign is uniform across the system, and every unit hexagon (square) has $0$-flux ($π$-flux) within the parameter space of interest. We map out the topological phase diagram for each model, which reveals parameter regimes hosting $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and Ising topological orders, and we derive an analytical expression for the mass term of the Majorana fermions, the vanishing of which indicates a transition between these phases. We further establish the correspondence between individual vortices (i.e., flux excitations) and two species of anyons in the dimer limit, overcoming the obstacle faced by degenerate perturbation theory in treating odd-length elementary plaquettes. Interestingly, we find that two dimer limits of the maple-leaf model with distinct assignments of anyon species can be smoothly connected to each other in the vortex-free sector, but they are separated by fermion-gap-closing transitions in certain two-vortex sectors.

</details>


### [2] [Dual Charge-Density-Waves in Two-dimensional DyTe3 and Their Distinct Impacts on Magneto-Transport Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04370)
*Shuvankar Gupta,Yasemin Ozbek,Olajumoke Oluwatobiloba Emmanuel,Maya Bostock,Johnathan Kowalski,Pengpeng Zhang,Xianglin Ke*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究发现稀土碲化物DyTe3中两个正交电荷密度波（CDW）对其电子输运性质产生截然不同的影响：CDW1转变下未观察到明显的磁输运异常，而CDW2的出现则导致磁阻急剧增强并诱发多带主导的非线性霍尔响应，表明DyTe3是理解准二维材料中多个CDW输运后果的理想模型体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷密度波是电子电荷密度周期性调制与晶格畸变耦合形成的宏观量子态，尽管已有大量研究，但在CDW存在条件下电磁输运性质的演化仍未被充分探索。

Method: 作者以DyTe3为研究对象，测量其在低温下共存的两个正交CDW（CDW1和CDW2）对电子输运特性的影响，包括磁阻和霍尔响应的测量。

Result: 实验发现，在当前实验条件下，CDW1转变过程中未观察到明确的磁输运异常；而CDW2的出现显著增强磁阻，并引发由多带输运驱动的非线性霍尔响应。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明DyTe3中的CDW1和CDW2本质上不同，二者对电子输运产生性质上有差异的影响，从而确立了DyTe3作为理解准二维材料中多个CDW输运后果的模型体系地位。

Abstract: Charge-density-waves (CDWs) are macroscopic quantum states defined by periodic modulations in electronic charge density coupled with lattice distortions. Despite significant research efforts, the evolution of electromagnetic transport properties in the presence of CDWs remains largely unexplored. Here, we report the effects of two orthogonal CDWs (CDW1 and CDW2) coexisting at low-temperature in DyTe3 on its electronic transport properties. Importantly, we find that while no clear magnetotransport anomaly is resolved across CDW1 transition under the present experimental conditions, the emergence of CDW2 drives a drastic enhancement in magnetoresistance and a multiband-governed nonlinear Hall response. These results demonstrate that CDW1 and CDW2 in DyTe3 are fundamentally different and impact its electronic transport in qualitatively distinct ways, establishing DyTe3 as a model system for understanding the transport consequences of multiple CDWs in quasi-two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [3] [Layer Architecture Shapes Electronic, Magnetic, and Lattice Interactions in Ruddlesden-Popper Nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04532)
*W. He,X. Guo,X. Luo,J. Thomas,J. Sears,Sophia F. R. TenHuisen,Ziqiang Guan,Xinglong Chen,D. A. Dahlbom,B. Zager,J. Pelliciari,Yi-Feng Zhao,H. LaBollita,Hong Zheng,M. K. Lajer,J. F. Mitchell,V. Bisogni,A. S. Botana,M. Mitrano,S. Johnston,M. P. M. Dean*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过镍L3边和氧K边的共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）对比研究了La3Ni2O7的两种多型结构（交替单层-三层LNO-1313与双层LNO-2222）以及三层化合物La4Ni3O10，发现LNO-1313与La4Ni3O10在电子、磁性和晶格激发上高度相似，而双层LNO-2222呈现显著不同的特征，并提出纠缠二聚体（entangled-dimer）框架可自然描述其自旋激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper镍基超导体的发现引发了核心问题：层状架构如何塑造与配对（pairing）相关的电子、磁性和晶格相互作用。现有研究缺乏对同组分不同层堆叠结构系统性的对比认识，需要厘清层结构对低能物理的影响。

Method: 采用镍L3边和氧K边的共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）技术，对La3Ni2O7的两种多型结构（LNO-1313和LNO-2222）以及La4Ni3O10进行系统性比较研究，分析其电子激发、磁激发与晶格激发的谱学特征，并运用有效局域矩（local-moment）框架对比纠缠二聚体模型与常规自旋波理论（spin-wave theory）的描述能力。

Result: LNO-1313与La4Ni3O10在电子、磁性和晶格激发上表现出高度相似性，而双层LNO-2222呈现独特特征。与LNO-2222相比，LNO-1313和La4Ni3O10具有更弱的轨道极化（orbital polarization）、更强的3d8L特征、更小的面外磁交换（out-of-plane magnetic-exchange）标度以及更强的电子-声子耦合（EPC）。在有效局域矩框架下，纠缠二聚体图景能自然描述由强反铁磁层间耦合产生的自旋激发，其优势在层间耦合主导层内相互作用的双层LNO-2222中最为明显。

Conclusion: 这些发现为建立层状镍酸盐超导电性相关低能物理的理论模型提供了关键实验约束，表明层堆叠结构对电子、磁性与晶格自由度具有深远的调控作用。

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates has raised a central question: how does layer architecture shape the electronic, magnetic, and lattice interactions relevant to pairing? Here, we report a detailed comparative study of the two polymorphs of La3Ni2O7--the alternating monolayer-trilayer (LNO-1313) and bilayer (LNO-2222) structures--and the related trilayer compound La4Ni3O10, using both Ni L3- and O K-edge RIXS. We find that LNO-1313 and La4Ni3O10 share strikingly similar electronic, magnetic, and lattice excitations, whereas bilayer LNO-2222 exhibits distinct features. Compared to LNO-2222, LNO-1313 and La4Ni3O10 have weaker orbital polarization, enhanced 3d8L character, a reduced out-of-plane magnetic-exchange scale, and stronger EPC. Within an effective local-moment framework, an entangled-dimer scenario provides a natural description of the spin excitations generated by strong antiferromagnetic interlayer coupling. Its advantage over conventional spin-wave theory is clearest in bilayer LNO-2222, where the interlayer coupling dominates the intralayer interactions. These findings provide critical experimental constraints for future theoretical models for the low-energy physics relevant to superconductivity in these layered nickelates.

</details>


### [4] [Environment-assisted transport in a strongly correlated boundary-driven Fermi-Hubbard chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05137)
*Hakan Yeler,Özgür Çakır*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用时间演化块退相干（TEBD）方法求解Lindblad主方程，研究了一维Fermi-Hubbard链在粒子库和局域退相干浴共同作用下的稳态输运。研究发现无边界退相干时电流随驱动率呈双峰结构，反映强关联区电荷与自旋的不同能量标度；引入退相干后出现两种不同的退相干诱导输运增强区域，与先前自旋less费米子研究中仅观察到的单一增强不同。通过哈密顿量本征基下的非平衡稳态分析，揭示两种增强分别源于Hubbard带内的高能带内重分布和带间跃迁两种不同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于开放量子系统中的退相干诱导输运增强现象主要针对无自旋费米子模型，强关联多体系统中这种效应是否呈现更丰富的结构尚不清楚。理解强关联系统中相干驱动、退相干与量子Zeno效应之间由多体关联调控的相互作用，对深入认识强关联开放系统的非平衡输运具有重要意义。

Method: 采用一维Fermi-Hubbard链模型，体系两端耦合粒子库（particle reservoirs），每个格点耦合局域退相干浴（local dephasing baths），并利用时间演化块退相干（TEBD）方法数值求解Lindblad主方程以获得稳态输运性质。同时在哈密顿量本征基下对非平衡稳态进行分析，以识别不同的退相干诱导重分布机制。

Result: 无退相干时，电流随边界驱动率呈现出两个分离良好的极大值，反映出强关联区域中电荷与自旋能量标度的差异。引入退相干后观察到两种截然不同的退相干诱导输运增强区域，这与此前在自旋less费米子中发现的单一增强现象形成对比。本征基分析表明，两种增强分别源于最上层Hubbard带内部的重分布以及不同Hubbard带之间的跃迁过程。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多体关联显著塑造了强关联开放系统中相干驱动、退相干与量子Zeno物理之间的相互作用，揭示了超出简单单带图像的两步退相干增强机制。该工作为理解强关联开放量子系统中的多体输运调控提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We study steady-state transport in a one-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard chain coupled to particle reservoirs at the boundaries and to local dephasing baths at each site, using the time-evolving block decimation (TEBD) method to solve the Lindblad master equation. In the absence of dephasing, the current exhibits two well-separated maxima as a function of the boundary driving rate, reflecting the distinct charge and spin energy scales of the strongly correlated regime. Upon introducing dephasing, we find two distinct dephasing-induced transport-enhancement regimes, in contrast to the single enhancement previously reported for spinless fermions. Analysis of the non-equilibrium steady state in the Hamiltonian eigenbasis reveals that the two regimes originate from distinct dephasing-induced redistribution processes: the first involves redistribution within the uppermost Hubbard band, while the second involves transitions between Hubbard bands. Our results demonstrate how many-body correlations shape the interplay between coherent driving, dephasing, and quantum Zeno physics in strongly correlated open systems.

</details>


### [5] [Dipolar and quadrupolar spin supersolid states in a spin-1 triangular antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05181)
*Yixuan Huang,Yuan Gao,Wei Li,Seiji Yunoki,Sadamichi Maekawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用密度矩阵重整化群（Density Matrix Renormalization Group, DMRG）方法，系统研究了三角晶格上自旋-1反铁磁海森堡模型在外平面磁场中的量子相图，识别出多种偶极型和四极型自旋超固态（spin supersolid）相，并在整个相图中计算了动力学自旋结构因子，为实验探测提供了理论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解受挫磁性系统中各向异性与塞曼相互作用（Zeeman interaction）如何共同驱动不同类型的自旋超固态相，并为相关三角晶格反铁磁材料（如Na₂BaNi(PO₄)₂和K₂Ni(SeO₃)₂）提供可被非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）实验验证的理论基础。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）数值方法，以单离子各向异性（single-ion anisotropy）D_z和磁场为参数，绘制自旋-1三角晶格反铁磁海森堡模型的量子相图；通过序参量和自旋超流刚度（spin superfluid stiffness）在不同系统尺寸下表征各相，并计算整个相图中的动力学自旋结构因子（dynamical spin structure factor）。

Result: 在零场下，具有"Y"型自旋构型的偶极型自旋超固态在D_z=0仍存在，而四极型自旋超固态在较大D_z下占主导；中磁场区间以up-up-down相为主；高场（低于饱和场）下，大D_z区域出现四极型自旋超流态，小D_z区域则为"V"型构型的偶极型自旋超固态。动力学自旋结构因子显示无间隙戈德斯通模（gapless Goldstone mode）和类旋子极小（roton-like minima）等特征谱。

Conclusion: 自旋超固态以自发U(1)对称性破缺伴随纵向平移对称性破缺为特征，即有限自旋超流刚度与纵向平移破缺共存。研究的谱学特征可直接被非弹性中子散射实验观测，为理解受挫效应、各向异性与塞曼相互作用在自旋-1系统中驱动不同自旋超固态相的竞争机制提供理论依据。

Abstract: We present a systematic numerical study of the spin-1 antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model on the triangular lattice in an out-of-plane magnetic field, using Density Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) methods. By mapping out the quantum phase diagram as a function of the single-ion anisotropy $D_z$ and magnetic field, we identify distinct dipolar and quadrupolar spin supersolid states, characterized by spontaneous U(1) symmetry breaking with finite spin superfluid stiffness coexisting with longitudinal translational symmetry breaking. At zero field, the dipolar spin supersolid with a 'Y'-type spin configuration persists down to $D_z = 0$, whereas the quadrupolar spin supersolid prevails at large $D_z$. At intermediate fields, the phase diagram is dominated by an up-up-down phase. At high fields below saturation, a quadrupolar spin superfluid emerges in the large-$D_z$ regime, whereas a dipolar spin supersolid with a 'V'-type spin configuration dominates at small $D_z$. These phases are characterized through their order parameters and spin superfluid stiffness using calculations on various system sizes. Furthermore, the dynamical spin structure factor is obtained across the phase diagram, where characteristic spectral signatures of different phases are observed, including the gapless Goldstone mode and the roton-like minima. These features are directly accessible to inelastic neutron scattering experiments. Our results provide a theoretical understanding of the interplay between frustrations, anisotropy, and Zeeman interactions in driving distinct spin supersolid phases in the spin-1 system, which are relevant to various triangular-lattice antiferromagnets such as Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ and K$_2$Ni(SeO$_3$)$_2$.

</details>


### [6] [Breakdown of Anomalous Hall Scaling in Dilute Kondo System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05319)
*Arnab Bhattacharya,Prashant Singh,Ajay Kumar,Afsar Ahmed,Yaroslav Mudryk,I. Das,Anis Biswas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本工作报道了非 $f$-电子Heusler合金 Ru$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$Ge 中由本征Berry曲率驱动的反常霍尔输运现象，该系统具有靠近费米能级的平带和 van Hove 奇点，且属于稀磁 Kondo 体系。反常霍尔响应呈显著的非单调行为，在磁转变附近偏离 Fermi-liquid 标度关系，仅在低温 Kondo 相干建立后才恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子关联与Berry曲率驱动的输运之间的交汇构成量子材料研究中尚未充分探索的前沿领域，尤其当电子关联源于平带物理而非传统 $f$-电子态时更缺乏研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，探讨平带Kondo相干性对拓扑性质输运的影响。

Method: 实验上测量了 Ru$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$Ge 的反常霍尔输运，考察其在磁转变附近的标度行为及低温Kondo相干建立后的恢复；理论上采用 ab initio 计算来验证Berry曲率的来源，揭示其源于Kondo杂化诱导的平带与自旋轨道耦合介导的反交叉相互作用。

Result: 反常霍尔响应呈现非单调特征，在磁转变附近违反 Fermi-liquid 标度关系，仅在低温、Kondo相干性建立后才恢复标度行为。第一性原理计算证实Berry曲率源于Kondo杂化导致的平带与自旋轨道耦合引发的费米面能级反交叉。

Conclusion: 研究表明，平带的Kondo相干性深刻影响与Berry曲率相关的铁性响应（ferroic responses），需要在倒空间拓扑与强电子关联内在交织的体系中建立对反常输运的理论理解框架。

Abstract: The confluence of strong electronic correlations and Berry curvature-driven transport constitutes a largely underexplored frontier in quantum materials research, particularly in systems where electronic correlations arise from flat-band physics rather than conventional $f$-electron states. Here, we report an intrinsic Berry curvature driven anomalous Hall transport in the dilute Kondo system Ru$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$Ge, a non-$f$-electron Heusler alloy hosting flat bands with van Hove singularity proximate to the Fermi level. The anomalous Hall response is strikingly non-monotonic, violating the Fermi-liquid scaling relation near the magnetic transition and is restored only at low temperature, coincident with the onset of Kondo coherence. \textit{Ab initio} calculations strongly establish that the Berry curvature originates from the Kondo hybridization induced interplay of flat bands and spin-orbit coupling mediated anticrossings at the Fermi level. Our findings demonstrate that Kondo coherence of flat bands bears a profound impact on Berry curvature associated ferroic responses, mandating a rigorous theoretical understanding of anomalous transport in the regime where reciprocal space topology and strong correlations are intrinsically intertwined.

</details>

### [7] [The influence of quantum geometry on the phase boundary and collective excitations of electron liquids and crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04330)
*Paul Froese,Mark R. Hirsbrunner,Yong Baek Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

Abstract: Identifying the origin of the superfluid stiffness of electron-electron and electron-hole pair condensates is an important issue in flat-band physics. The stiffness of condensates formed between moiré flat bands, which typically have strong variations in Berry curvature and quantum metric across their Brillouin zones, tends to be larger when the bands have non-zero Chern numbers and can be larger than that of Landau levels. Abstract: Naturally occurring bulk quasicrystals are rare, and magnetic instances are rarer still, with chemical constraints typically permitting the synthesis of approximants rather than true quasicrystalline magnets. Together, these properties provide a model two-dimensional quasicrystalline magnet, with magnetic correlations that we probe with magnetic force microscopy and parallel-tempered Monte Carlo simulations. We identify a two-stage partial ordering process, driven by the competition between two possible positions for magnetic excitations. This yields an unusual type of medium-range order, where the underlying quasiperiodic long-range order is randomly modulated by a strictly constrained disorder. Our findings establish the Einstein artificial spin ice as a blueprint for understanding quasicrystalline magnetism, demonstrating how quasiperiodic monotile geometries can be exploited to engineer unconventional magnetic phases with no direct equivalent in periodic systems.

</details>

Abstract: Multipole moments provide a unified symmetry language for describing electronic degrees of freedom and their associated physical responses. Furthermore, by classifying response tensors within the same framework, we establish a correspondence between active multipoles and symmetry-allowed physical responses. This correspondence systematically distinguishes nonrelativistic responses that survive without spin-orbit coupling from those requiring relativistic effects, providing a unified framework for understanding and predicting spin-dependent electromagnetic and transport phenomena in magnetic materials.

Conclusion: 强非局域性与半导体二维电子井中实验可及的漂移速度相结合，将抛物线色散系统确定为实现可调谐非互易等离激元学的有前景平台。

Abstract: We develop a nonlocal electrodynamic framework for drift-biased two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs) with Dirac (linear) and parabolic (quadratic) dispersions, deriving closed-form drift-dependent conductivity tensors from the Boltzmann transport equation. By replacing a process that previously demanded days of custom scripting with a point-and-click workflow, ANT.UI accelerates research in theoretical molecular electronics and lowers the barrier to entry for new users. Magnetic flux further reconstructs the transmission spectrum through Aharonov-Bohm interference, driving a crossover of the optimal thermoelectric regime from the trivial to the topological dimerized phase. Under optimal flux conditions, the thermoelectric figure of merit reaches $ZT \approx 12$ for symmetric coupling and is dramatically enhanced to $ZT \approx 90$ for asymmetric coupling through enhanced energy filtering and suppressed electronic thermal transport. We further establish a clear correlation between the enhancement of thermoelectric efficiency and the violation of the Wiedemann-Franz law. Our results demonstrate that the combined interplay of quasiperiodicity, topology, magnetic flux, and electrode-coupling geometry provides a versatile strategy for engineering high-performance coherent thermoelectric devices.

Combining dc and rf measurements, we unambiguously evidence the signature of both integer ($ν= 1$ and $2$) and fractional quantum Hall states ($ν= 4/3$ and $2/3$) within the radiofrequency transmission signal.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Weyl semimetals (WSMs), with their unique topological properties and distinct electronic structure, exhibit intriguing properties when either time-reversal or inversion symmetries are broken. In this work, we consider the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interaction between magnetic impurities in time-reversal symmetry-breaking WSMs. We derive analytical expressions for the full RKKY exchange tensor in arbitrary two-band spinful lattice systems. Our approach reveals both Heisenberg, anisotropic Ising and Dzyaloshinsky-Moriya terms, which can be calculated by energy-integrating real-space Green's functions across the entire Brillouin zone, with the band edge acting as a natural energy cutoff. We apply this framework to study a two-band tight-binding model for a time-reversal symmetry-breaking WSM that interpolates between a Weyl phase with well-separated chiral nodes and a quadratic band-touching semimetal phase. Remarkably, the spatial profile, magnitude, and anisotropic tensor structure of the exchange couplings remain persistent across the node-annihilation transition. This topological robustness reveals that short- and intermediate-range RKKY interactions are mediated by the global, Brillouin-zone-integrated quantum metric of the full valence band rather than being strictly dictated by local low-energy Berry curvature monopoles. These findings demonstrate the necessity of full-band tight-binding formulations when predicting real-space magnetic interactions, providing key insights for electric-field tuning of magnetic anisotropy and constructing realistic models of heavy-fermion and Weyl-Kondo semimetals.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Using low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy we investigate the charge state, magnetic excitations, and Kondo features of individual Sm adatoms on graphene/Ir(111). Depending on the number and distance of their neighbors, Sm atoms can be in two discrete charge states. At certain distances, a reversible transition between these two states is induced by the electric field of the STM tip leading to concentric charge rings in the images. Only atoms in one of the two charge states exhibit magnetic excitations in d$I$/d$V$ spectra. Two such excitations are located at 35~meV and 54~meV and related to transitions from the $J = 1/2$ ground state doublet to the first crystal field split $J = 3/2$ multiplet. Together with the intra-atomic exchange excitations at higher energy, these observations indicate that Sm transfers one $6s$ electron to the substrate while it retains its gas-phase $4f$ filling. New for lanthanide adatoms, we observe a Kondo resonance. The Zeeman splitting of the Kondo peak reveals that Sm retains its large gas-phase $g$-factor. Comparison of d$I$/d$V$ spectra to cotunneling theory yields the crystal field acting on the $4f$ shell and, consequently, on the $J = 3/2$ quadruplet, confirms Sm$^+$ as the ground state, and identifies Sm$^{2+}$ as being energetically close, thereby rationalizing our observation of variable charge states.

</details>

### [21] [Data-Driven Discovery of Composition-Dependent Constitutive Models for Hyperelasticity and Viscoelasticity of Digital Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04541)
*Josué García-Ávila,Beijun Shen,Manuel K. Rausch,Mary C. Boyce,Adrián Buganza-Tepole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的多材料本构建模框架，推广了Bergström–Boyce模型，通过平衡分支的闭式参数辨识或基于神经常微分方程（NODEs）的自动凸弹性构建，以及非平衡分支的动力学学习，实现跨材料组成和加载速率对率相关刚度与滞回行为的统一表征，同时保持热力学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由多材料3D打印制造的数字材料表现出跨数量级的表观刚度差异以及强非线性、组成依赖和率依赖的耗散行为，而经典有限应变粘弹性模型依赖闭式应变能函数和内变量演化关系，当单一本构模型需跨材料和加载率泛化时灵活性受限。

Method: 框架保留经典模型结构（乘法运动学分解、不变量基应变能函数、沿归一化非平衡偏应力方向的标量耗散演化律）；平衡分支通过直接预测组成函数的闭式参数或利用NODEs自动构造多凸应变能函数实现数据驱动发现；非平衡分支动力学通过跨组成闭式参数辨识或约束人工神经网络（ANNs）学习。

Result: 利用多材料组成下的多速率单轴压缩数据验证，结果表明该框架能够捕获跨组成的率相关刚度和滞回行为，并同时维持热力学一致性。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动框架在保留经典粘弹性模型物理结构的同时，通过组成感知的闭式参数预测或神经函数自动构造成分依赖的应变能与非平衡动力学，为多材料3D打印数字材料的本构建模提供灵活且热力学一致性的统一方案。

Abstract: Digital materials fabricated by multi-material 3D printing are designed as controlled mixtures of stiff and compliant constituents, yielding effective responses that span more than an order of magnitude in apparent stiffness and exhibit strongly nonlinear, composition-dependent, and rate-dependent dissipative behavior. Classical finite-strain viscoelastic models represent such behavior with closed-form strain energy functions for equilibrium and non-equilibrium stresses as well as evolution of internal variables, which may limit flexibility when a single constitutive model is expected to generalize across materials and loading rates. Here, we present a data-driven multi-material constitutive modeling framework that generalizes a formulation by Bergström and Boyce. The proposed framework retains the structure of the classical model, namely multiplicative kinematics, invariant-based strain-energy functions, and a scalar dissipative evolution law directed along the normalized nonequilibrium deviatoric stress. For the equilibrium branch, the data-driven discovery framework either directly predicts closed-form model parameters as functions of composition or automatically constructs a polyconvex strain-energy function using neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs). The nonequilibrium branch kinetics are learned similarly, either by directly identifying closed-form parameters across compositions or by using appropriately constrained artificial neural networks. Using multi-rate uniaxial compression data across multiple material compositions, we show that the proposed formulation captures rate-dependent stiffness and hysteresis across compositions while preserving thermodynamic consistency.

Abstract: The field of gravitational wave "dark siren" cosmology is facing a number of challenges surrounding the way in which galaxy catalogue data is incorporated into the analysis. The superconducting critical temperature of Al varies systematically with the film surface morphology and grain size. In addition, the critical magnetic field of Al exhibits an anomalous decrease below 0.5 K, possibly reflecting magnetic coupling to the underlying FMS (In,Fe)As layer. These findings provide a guideline for material design of epitaxial Al/(In,Fe)As heterostructures, which may serve as a promising platform for investigating proximity-induced superconducting and magnetic phenomena in semiconductor-based hybrid quantum devices.

Abstract: We study the stability of the solution corresponding to the polar phase of superfluid 3He in nematic aerogels with respect to perturbations induced by magnetic impurity scattering. We show that when the perturbation simultaneously breaks axial and time-reversal symmetry, the polar-distorted A phase is realized with the highest transition temperature. Within the proposed model of a p-wave scattering potential, which accounts for the effective spin-orbit interaction between the orbital angular momentum of a scattering quasiparticle and the magnetic moment texture of the scatterer, we solve the eigenvalue and eigenvector problem for the Gor'kov self-consistency equation. Finally, we discuss how our results relate to the available experimental data.

Result: 证明了串联两结的不对称性和外加磁通偏置可对rf-SQUID势能的局域展开提供独立控制，从而找到前导四阶Kerr项被抑制而三次非线性保持有限的运行点；推导了相应的无Kerr条件并界定了参数空间中物理可行的工作区域。

We employ a full-spectrum model based on orthonormalized projections of Kohn-Sham states onto local Ni-$e_g$ orbitals, which preserves the density-functional band structure while redistributing spectral weight over a wide energy range. Compared to a low-energy description, this approach yields enhanced interlayer spin fluctuations and a commensurate magnetic instability. Within a spin-fluctuation framework, these features favor a sign-changing $s^\pm$ superconducting state, whereas low-energy models tend to stabilize $d$-wave pairing. Our results suggest that interlayer coupling in full-energy models may play an important role in shaping the predicted pairing symmetry of bilayer nickelates.

The full resistively and capacitively shunted junction equations are compared with an effective synthetic element with tunable transparency that retains the synthetic tunable-transparency current-phase relation together with effective capacitive and dissipative terms, thus reducing the two second order degree of freedom system to a single second order degree of freedom. The agreement is quantified through a normalized root-mean-square error between the full and effective voltage waveforms. A broad low-error region is found at low drive frequency, while pronounced deviations emerge as the drive frequency approaches the relevant plasma-frequency scale and at larger drive amplitudes.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Q流形（即配备奇向量场且该场平方为零的超流形）的框架下重新审视Loday的导出乘积，并尝试将Connes循环上同调方法推广到这一非交换结合代数设定中，以建立新的上同调理论。

Method: 将Grassmann奇乘积（Grassmann odd product）在平移分次后解释为标准乘积，以此证明任何Q流形都对应一个导出的（非交换）结合Z₂-分次代数；随后对该导出结合代数应用Connes循环上同调，构造称为导出循环上同调的Q流形新上同调。

Result: 建立了Q流形的导出循环上同调（derived cyclic cohomology），该上同调区别于标准上同调；所得导出循环上闭链（derived cyclic cocycles）在BV-BFV-BRST形式中被解释为经典BRST不变泛函（BRST-invariant functionals），在正则叶状结构（regular foliations）情形下通过叶状李代数胚（foliation Lie algebroids）被解释为广义Ruelle-Sullivan流（Ruelle–Sullivan currents）。

Abstract: Loday's derived product is revisited in the setting of Q-manifolds, i.e., supermanifolds equipped with an odd vector field that `squares to zero'. We interpret the Grassmann odd product as a standard product upon shifting the grading, which implies the existence of a derived (noncommutative) associative $\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded algebra associated with any Q-manifold. We apply Connes' cyclic cohomology to the derived associative algebra, giving a new cohomology on Q-manifolds distinct from the standard cohomology: we refer to this as the derived cyclic cohomology of a Q-manifold. The derived cyclic cocycles are interpreted as classically BRST-invariant functionals within the BV--BFV--BRST formalism or generalised Ruelle--Sullivan currents when applied to regular foliations via their foliation Lie algebroids.

Combining this reconstruction principle with finite-dimensional separation and the Choi representation, we show that non-membership in a global cone is always detected on a single finite-dimensional corner by a finite-dimensional witness. We illustrate the framework for completely positive and decomposable maps; in the decomposable case, the Hermitian parts of the finite-corner witnesses are PPT.

Abstract: A search is presented for the rare, loop-induced Higgs boson decay H $\to$ Z$γ$, where Z $\to$ $\ell^+\ell^-$ and $\ell$ = e or $μ$. The search is performed using a sample of proton-proton (pp) collision data at the center-of-mass energies of 13 and 13.6 TeV, recorded by the CMS experiment at the LHC and corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 200 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis design separately considers and optimizes sensitivity to Higgs boson production via gluon-gluon fusion, vector boson fusion, and associated-production processes. The signal is extracted from a simultaneous fit to the invariant mass distributions of the $\ell^+\ell^-γ$ system in the various production channels and event categories. The observed (expected) signal strength $μ$, defined as the ratio of the measured product the of cross section and decay branching fraction value $σ$(pp $\to$ H)$\mathcal{B}$(H $\to$ Z$γ$) to the corresponding value predicted in the standard model, is found to be $μ$ = 1.10$^{+0.52}_{-0.61}$ (1.00$^{+0.49}_{-0.46}$) for a Higgs boson mass of 125.38 GeV. The signal has an observed (expected) significance of 1.9 (2.3) standard deviations.

Abstract: The photocurrent of an organic solar cell is routinely plotted against an effective voltage and used to extract an exciton dissociation probability, and through it a free-charge generation efficiency. The photocurrent measures neither. Across wide variations of carrier mobility and recombination strength it is a single-valued function of the fill factor and coincides with the short-circuit charge collection efficiency, reporting neither the generation yield nor anything about collection beyond what the fill factor already shows. Even for ideal transport the normalised photocurrent saturates below unity at an intensity-independent first-order limit set by recombination of photogenerated carriers with injected equilibrium charge. Applied to four organic solar cells whose generation efficiencies are known independently, the apparent dissociation probabilities track the short-circuit collection efficiency rather than the generation yield. </details>

Abstract: The capillary fluctuation method (CFM) is widely used to compute solid--liquid interfacial properties from atomistic simulations, but its accuracy depends on choices in interface construction, wave-vector selection, sampling, and simulation geometry. Here, we develop a diagnostics-driven workflow for reproducible CFM calculations using pure Al as a representative system. Employing both ribbon models and thick two-dimensional references, we show that apparent linearity of the fluctuation spectrum alone does not ensure reliable stiffness or anisotropy estimates. Instead, a reliable CFM analysis requires a fitting window consistent with both temporal sampling and continuum capillary-wave assumptions, systematic sensitivity tests of the interface identification procedure, explicit propagation of replica variability, and independent verification of model-thickness convergence. We further propose a practical thickness-selection rule based on coexistence-temperature consistency, which enables finite-size effects to be controlled while retaining the substantial computational efficiency of ribbon geometries. By making the main sources of uncertainty explicit and diagnosable, the proposed workflow improves the reliability of the CFM as a quantitative tool and provides a foundation for its broader application to complex solid--liquid interfaces.

Conventional scalar sparse formats store the entries of each block individually, obscuring this local structure and limiting the use of efficient block algorithms. A unified interface automatically maps scalar, uniform-basis, and multispecies operators to compressed sparse row (CSR), block sparse row (BSR), or variable-block compressed sparse row (VBCSR). Our advanced acceleration method groups blocks of equal shape and dispatches them to optimized dense kernels. In the reported benchmarks, VBCSR outperforms the tested Python-accessible reference implementations for several block-sparse benchmarks. We further demonstrate VBCSR in an InP nanoparticle application containing more than \(10^6\) atoms.

Abstract: Triboelectric nanogenerators (TENGs) incorporating metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) have largely been designed around porous architectures, based on the assumption that high internal surface area is the primary driver for triboelectric enhancement. These features are accompanied by reduced work of adhesion and modified surface roughness, hence improving contact electrification. These findings establish dense MOFs as an effective triboelectric filler and identify interfacial electronic structure and polarization as key design parameters for engineering mechanical energy harvesters and self-powered sensors.

Abstract: This work describes the calculation of spin-orbit spillage from a crystal structure. Spin-orbit spillage provides a measure of the likelihood that a material has topological character. Abstract: Reduced exciton mass ($μ$) was recently reported to correlate strongly with a framework distortion in a series of nine single-layer (2D) metal-halide perovskite (HOIP) compounds. Abstract: This work presents a density functional theory (DFT) investigation of a two-dimensional (2D) C5N monolayer functionalized with Li, Na, and K for hydrogen storage. Ab initio molecular dynamics simulations further confirm the thermal stability of the functionalized systems at 300 K. Charge transfer from the metal dopants to C5N enhances polarization and strengthens H2 adsorption. Each dopant can adsorb up to eight H2 molecules, yielding a maximum of 48 H2 molecules per unit cell and gravimetric storage capacities of 9.42, 8.61, and 7.93 wt% for Li-, Na-, and K-functionalized C5N, respectively. The average H2 adsorption energies of -0.16 to -0.17 eV/H2 indicate moderate interactions suitable for reversible storage. Thermodynamic analysis further demonstrates favourable H2 adsorption/desorption under practical operating conditions, while desorption-temperature, recovery-time, and volumetric analyses support the potential reversibility and storage performance of these systems. Overall, alkali-metal-functionalized C5N emerges as a promising 2D material for efficient and reversible H2 storage.

Abstract: Recently synthesized carbon nanoribbons (CNRs) were investigated for hydrogen (H2) storage using first-principles density functional theory calculations. The maximum theoretical gravimetric storage capacities at 0 K reached 7.48 wt% for the Mn-doped system and 6.55 wt% for the Y-doped system. Under practical conditions of 30 atm and 298.15 K, the storage capacity of the Y-doped system decreased to 6.04 wt%, whereas the Mn-doped system maintained its full capacity of 7.48 wt%. Thermodynamic analysis indicated that H2 adsorption is favored at low temperatures and high pressures, while desorption becomes feasible at elevated temperatures and lower pressures. These results demonstrate that Mn- and Y-doped CNRs satisfy key U.S. Department of Energy requirements for reversible H2 storage and show promise as potential hydrogen storage materials under near-ambient conditions.

Abstract: A field-driven steepest-entropy-ascent quantum thermodynamic (SEAQT) formulation is developed for longitudinal nonequilibrium relaxation in magnetite (Fe$_3$O$_4$) with coupled electron, phonon, and magnon populations. Material-specific excitation spectra define the thermodynamic state space, while one relaxation parameter for each population sets its kinetic scale. A longitudinal magnetic field shifts the dressed magnon eigenenergies while the occupation basis remains fixed; irreversible redistribution conserves instantaneous energy and electron number while allowing the magnon population to vary. The formulation yields nonequilibrium subsystem temperatures, entropy production, magnetic-work identities, and a coupled small-signal susceptibility incorporating energy-conservation feedback among all three populations; the one-pole Debye response appears only as a limiting case. Numerical results under sinusoidal driving show a transition from nearly quasistatic behavior to frequency-dependent lag, finite-amplitude departure from the linear-response ellipse, and increasing higher-harmonic content. Relaxational work per cycle increases strongly with field amplitude and frequency, while the complex susceptibility is broader and shifted relative to a Debye reference. Entropy production remains positive, and the electron, phonon, and magnon temperatures show distinct excursions followed by secular heating when positive magnetic work is retained without heat rejection. The calculated work represents longitudinal magnon quasiparticle relaxation in a homogeneous single-domain model, not the total core loss of a finite ferrite specimen.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电子学的最新进展表明，电荷注入轻金属产生的轨道霍尔电流可以在不依赖强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）的情况下提供非平衡角动量。本文旨在检验这种轨道电流能否驱动非晶铁磁合金中的磁畴壁运动，探究轨道霍尔效应在磁畴壁操控中的应用可行性。

Method: 选取三种具有代表性的异质结构进行对比研究：Pt/GFC作为自旋霍尔参考体系，轻金属（Mn或Ti）/GFC用于直接轨道电流注入，以及轻金属/Pt/GFC通过在界面插入超薄Pt层实现轨道—自旋转换。利用二次谐波霍尔测量表征阻尼类力矩（damping-like torque），并结合厚度依赖分析研究畴壁迁移率与去钉扎阈值的变化规律。

Result: Pt/GFC结构中观测到稳定且可重复的自旋轨道力矩驱动的畴壁运动，而Mn/GFC和Ti/GFC中均未检测到电流驱动的畴壁运动。尽管二次谐波霍尔测量显示Mn/GFC和Ti/GFC中确实存在有限的阻尼类力矩，表明角动量确实转移至GFC，但其强度远弱于Pt/GFC。在界面插入1纳米厚Pt转换层后，阻尼类力矩显著增强并恢复畴壁运动。厚度依赖分析进一步揭示，畴壁迁移率和去钉扎阈值与界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用和畴壁宽度相关。

Abstract: Recent advances in spintronics suggest that orbital Hall currents generated by charge injection in light metals can provide nonequilibrium angular momentum without relying on strong spin-orbit coupling (SOC). Here, we examine whether such orbital currents can drive domain wall (DW) motion in an amorphous ferrimagnetic alloy, Gd$_{25}$(Fe$_{9}$Co$_{1}$)$_{75}$ (GFC), where the rare-earth sublattice offers strong SOC for orbital-to-spin conversion. We compare three representative heterostructures: Pt/GFC as a spin Hall reference, light-metal (Mn or Ti)/GFC for direct orbital-current injection, and light-metal/Pt/GFC incorporating an ultrathin Pt layer for orbital-to-spin conversion. Whereas Pt/GFC exhibits robust and reproducible spin-orbit-torque-driven DW motion, no current-driven DW motion is detected in Mn/GFC or Ti/GFC. Second-harmonic Hall measurements nevertheless reveal finite damping-like torques in both Mn/GFC and Ti/GFC, demonstrating that angular-momentum transfer into GFC does occur but is far weaker than in Pt/GFC. Inserting a 1-nm-thick Pt conversion layer strongly enhances the damping-like torque and restores DW motion. Thickness-dependent analysis further shows that DW mobility and depinning thresholds correlate with the interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction and domain-wall width, highlighting weak torque conversion and insufficient interfacial stabilization of chiral DWs as key challenges for orbital-driven DW motion in light-metal/ferrimagnet heterostructures.

Method: 作者提出一种生成式多任务深度学习（GMTDL）框架，通过对多个任务间数据集知识的充分挖掘与共享，克服数据集局限性和不平衡性带来的限制；该模型不仅能同时预测多个目标性能，还能对合金成分进行调控与生成，为进一步实验处理提供候选材料。模型的有效性通过近期报道的铁基非晶合金基准（benchmarking）进行严格验证。

Result: GMTDL在具备多目标性能合金的预测方面表现出优于以往机器学习设计策略的精度；基准测试验证了模型的可靠性与有效性。此外，模型预测出若干具有超高B_s和超低H_c的新型合金，并解析出关键元素的最佳含量窗口及其协同效应，为实际成分设计提供了可操作的指导。

Conclusion: 这项研究为同时预测和优化具有多性能指标的高性能材料建立了一套有效且可靠的范式（paradigm），有望推广至多目标材料设计的更广泛场景。

Abstract: Fe-based amorphous alloys are promising soft magnetic materials for developing next-generation devices with high frequency and efficiency. However, optimization of Fe-based alloys with ultra-high saturation magnetic flux density (B_s), ultra-low coercivity (H_c), and good glass-forming ability is a notorious problem, owing to the vast composition space and complex trade-offs of these properties. Thus, conventional design methods encounter great challenges. Here we develop a generative multi-task deep learning (GMTDL) to achieve simultaneous optimization of compositions and trade-off properties. The GMTDL can sufficiently exploit and share the knowledge of datasets across different tasks, despite the limitation and imbalance of these datasets. Therefore, it exhibits superior performance in prediction of alloys with multiple targeted properties, outperforming previous machine learning-based design strategies. Moreover, the GMTDL can also tailor compositions, providing an efficient way to regulate properties and generate desired candidates for further experimental processing. The validity and reliability of GMTDL are rigorously tested by benchmarking with Fe-based alloys reported very recently. Moreover, some new alloys with ultra-high B_s and ultra-low H_c are predicted. The optimal content windows of key elements and their synergistic effects are also unraveled for practical guidance. Thus, our study establishes an effective and reliable paradigm for simultaneous prediction and optimization of high-performance materials with multiple properties.

### [51] [Nuclear quantum effects in the thermal conductivity of solid hydrogen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04955)
*Hengtai Zheng,Zezhu Zeng,David E. Manolopoulos*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The Nielsen-Ninomiya theorem dictates that Weyl nodes must appear in pairs of opposite chirality to preserve global charge neutrality. Abstract: We investigated the thermoelectric effects of the Dirac electron system Ag2Te under magnetic field.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在拉伸、压缩和剪切加载下对Al/Al3Ti、Al/Al9M2和Al9M2/Al3Ti三种界面进行了验证。拉伸加载产生最大的界面容纳，剪切加载结构演化有限；Al9M2/Al3Ti界面兼具最高的屈服阻力和最大的容纳响应。

Abstract: Heterophase interfaces are increasingly recognized as active participants in plastic deformation, yet quantitative methods for comparing their strain-accommodation capacity remain limited. Here, we present an atomistic framework for quantifying interface-mediated strain accommodation using molecular dynamics simulations. Interface broadening, roughening, and migration are extracted from atomistic trajectories and combined into an Interface Accommodation Index, with a normalized counterpart accounting for differences in initial interface structure. A weighting sensitivity analysis demonstrates that the relative ranking of interfaces is robust to the specific form of the index. The framework is demonstrated for three experimentally motivated interfaces, Al/Al3Ti, Al/Al9M2, and Al9M2/Al3Ti (M = Fe, Co, Ni), under tensile, compressive, and shear loading. Bulk simulations show that Shockley partial dislocations dominate plastic deformation, with alloy chemistry governing the transition toward mixed-character dislocation networks. Tensile loading produces the greatest interface accommodation, while shear produces comparatively limited structural evolution. The Al9M2/Al3Ti interface exhibits both the highest yield resistance and the largest accommodation response, suggesting that intermetallic-intermetallic interfaces can simultaneously sustain load and redistribute strain. These simulations quantify structural accommodation rather than ductility or fracture directly; their connection to macroscopic ductility therefore requires experimental validation. The framework provides a transferable approach for comparing interface accommodation in multiphase alloys and identifies interface chemistry and crystallography as important design variables for damage-tolerant structural materials.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

In this work rather than modifying the transformed tensor we seek the best performing cloak within the class of Cauchy materials. We formulate 2D Rayleigh wave carpet cloak design as an optimisation problem governed by partial differential equations. Using a coordinate based neural-field and a differentiable finite element model solver we optimise symmetric stiffness and density fields by minimising wave field distortion. Both single frequency and broadband optimisation are considered, with the broadband model trained over multiple frequencies. Physical realisability is addressed using a database of homogenised microstructures through conditional diffusion, neural-field inverse design, and nearest-neighbour selection. FEM simulations show that the optimised Cauchy design approaches the ideal transformation-based cloak. After projection onto explicit microstructures, the homogenised representation recovers approximately 97% of the defect free reference surface-displacement magnitude, while direct FEM simulation of the fully resolved microstructured geometry recovers approximately 76%

The combination of polarized neutron reflectometry and nuclear resonant scattering enables precise and direct determination of the depth-dependent spin configurations. Abstract: Glyphosate is a widely used herbicide whose persistence and toxicity in aquatic and terrestrial environments demand efficient removal strategies. Decomposition of the solvation free energy shows dominant electrostatic stabilization complemented by increasingly favorable hydrogen bond contributions as carboxyl density grows. All complexes display very small HOMO-LUMO gaps (0.02-0.22 eV), indicating high electronic sensitivity to adsorption and functionalization. Overall, CNT+COOH systems can operate in both strong capture and regenerable regimes depending on glyphosate ionization state, offering a tunable platform for pH responsive remediation.

</details>

### [65] [Low electrical conductivity of dry CaSiO3 perovskite under lower mantle conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04499)
*Yoshiyuki Okuda,Bin Chen,Juliana Peckenpaugh,Hirokazu Kadobayashi*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用激光加热金刚石压砧（laser-heated diamond anvil cell）中的阻抗谱学（impedance spectroscopy）技术，测量了标称干燥的CaSiO3钙钛矿（davemaoite）在高达89 GPa和2200 K条件下的电导率（electrical conductivity）。研究发现其电导率随温度升高而增大、随压力升高而系统降低，显著低于此前报道值，且在下地幔地温梯度下无法解释冷俯冲板片中的高电导异常，表明干燥davemaoite对下地幔整体电导贡献有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电导率是约束地球深部下地幔组成与挥发分分布的重要物理参量，而作为下地幔主要矿物之一，CaSiO3钙钛矿（davemaoite）的电导率此前缺乏可靠约束，限制了人们对下地幔电性结构与物质组成的理解。

Method: 作者在激光加热金刚石压砧装置中结合阻抗谱学方法，对标称干燥（nominally dry）的CaSiO3钙钛矿样品在高达89 GPa压力和高达2200 K温度条件下进行了原位电导率测量。

Result: 电导率随温度升高而增加、随压力升高而系统性降低，且明显低于此前报道的数值，与近期基于氧空位（oxygen vacancy）介导离子输运的理论预测大体一致。在四方相到立方相（tetragonal-to-cubic）相边界处，温度依赖关系出现明显改变，表明结构转变伴随离子输运的适度增强。在正常下地幔地温曲线上，干燥davemaoite在靠近下地幔顶部时电导率与bridgmanite相当，但随着深度增加而逐渐变得更不导电；在冷俯冲板片条件下，其电导率远低于干燥俯冲MORB，无法解释观测到的高电导异常。

Conclusion: 干燥davemaoite对下地幔整体电导率的贡献很小，此前关于其电导率的高估可能需要重新审视，这为下地幔电性结构和物质组成的解释提供了新的定量约束。

Abstract: Electrical conductivity (EC) provides important constraints on the composition and volatile distribution of Earth's deep mantle, yet the EC of davemaoite (CaSiO3 perovskite), a major lower-mantle phase, remains poorly constrained. We measured the EC of nominally dry CaSiO3 perovskite at pressures up to 89 GPa and temperatures up to 2200 K using impedance spectroscopy in a laser-heated diamond anvil cell. Conductivity increases with temperature but decreases systematically with pressure and it is substantially lower than previously reported, yet broadly consistent with recent theoretical predictions for oxygen-vacancy-mediated ionic transport. A distinct change in the temperature dependence coincides with the tetragonal-to-cubic phase boundary, revealing a modest enhancement of ionic transport across the structural transition. Along a normal lower-mantle geotherm, dry davemaoite is comparable in conductivity to bridgmanite near the top of the lower mantle but becomes progressively less conductive with depth. Under cold-slab conditions, dry davemaoite is substantially less conductive than dry subducted MORB and cannot account for the observed high-conductivity anomalies. Dry davemaoite therefore contributes little to bulk lower-mantle conductivity.

</details>

*Kevin A. Gonzalez,Nicolas H. Toledo,David A. Miranda*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统的实验设计检验了三电极电化学阻抗谱（EIS）中对电极（CE）独立性假设，发现纳米结构电极（TiO₂ + rGO）体系中测量的三电极电容响应依赖于CE特性，该现象无法由经典电路行为解释，但与受量子失协（quantum discord）启发的有效电极间关联框架兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典三电极操作下，工作电极（WE）与参比电极（RE）之间的阻抗/电容响应应当独立于对电极（CE）的性质（在常规电化学和仪器伪迹可忽略的条件下）。作者旨在实证检验这一经典预期是否适用于纳米结构电极体系。

Method: 作者在含有纳米结构电极TiO₂ + rGO的电解池中开展实验，采用多种对照手段包括：虚拟电池（dummy-cell）测量、双电极 bar-bar 对照、Pt 与 bar CE 的比较、在CE支路中引入虚拟电路，以及CE整体比较，以区分加和电路行为与三电极配置特有的CE依赖性行为。

Result: 实验发现，所测量的三电极电容响应确实依赖于CE，该效应无法由所检验的虚拟电路对照重现。这表明存在宏观的CE依赖性电容响应，偏离了所检验的经典预期。

Conclusion: 实验发现与由量子失协启发的有效电极间关联框架相容，但电容证据本身并不构成对量子失协的直接测量，仍需进一步验证其物理本质。

Abstract: In three-electrode electrochemical impedance spectroscopy (EIS), the impedance/capacitance response of the working electrode (WE) is measured relative to the reference electrode (RE), whereas the current required by potentiostatic control flows between the WE and the counter electrode (CE). Under classical three-electrode operation, the measured WE-RE response should therefore be independent of the properties of the electrode used as the CE, provided that conventional electrochemical and instrumental artifacts are negligible. Here, we test this expectation in cells containing nanostructured electrodes TiO2 + rGO and examine the results within a framework motivated by quantum discord to describe effective interelectrode correlations. We used dummy-cell measurements, a two-electrode bar-bar control, comparisons between Pt and bar CEs, a dummy circuit introduced into the CE branch, and CE ensemble comparisons to distinguish additive circuit behavior from CE-dependent behavior specific to the three-electrode configuration. In these experiments, the measured three-electrode capacitance response depends on CE, an effect that is not reproduced by the dummy-circuit controls examined. These findings identify a macroscopic CE-dependent capacitance response that departs from the tested classical expectation and is compatible with an effective interelectrode-correlation framework motivated by quantum discord, although the capacitance evidence alone does not constitute a direct measurement of quantum discord.

</details>


### [67] [Recovering molecules from coarse-grained beads: free-energy-conditioned generative backmapping across chemical space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04432)
*Luis Itza Vazquez-Salazar,Tristan Bereau*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 compositonal backmapping（成分回映）问题，将其形式化为以辛醇—水分配自由能 ΔG^{CG}_{W→O} 为条件的分子图条件生成任务，并引入 juniper——一种基于分子图的离散去噪扩散模型（discrete denoising diffusion model），可将粗粒化（coarse-grained, CG）珠子组合反推为具体候选分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可迁移的粗粒化力场（如 MARTINI 模型）将原子聚合为少量相互作用珠子，压缩化学空间约三个数量级，使得热力学性质的高通量筛选可行；但这种压缩是有损且单向的——筛选结果只返回珠子组合，缺乏回到其对应化合物的途径，即组成回映（compositional backmapping）问题尚未被解决，且不同于已熟知的构象回映问题。

Method: 作者将组成回映形式化为条件图生成问题，设计 juniper——一个以辛醇—水分配自由能 ΔG^{CG}_{W→O}（MARTINI 珠子类型分配的主要驱动因素，即珠子身份的代理）为条件、作用于分子图上的离散去噪扩散模型。训练数据为映射到一个或两个珠子、含至多 9 个重原子的分子。

Result: 对于双珠子目标，juniper 生成的分子具有 93\% 的化学有效率和 92\% 的独特性，且其 ΔG^{CG}_{W→O} 分布与目标值呈线性相关（r² ≥ 0.96），仅在中疏水性和亲水性两端略有偏离。虽模型只接收单个标量作为化学输入，但其生成的功能基团随施加的自由能系统性地从支链烃（疏水端）向酰胺、酰亚胺和异氰酸酯（极性端）偏移。

Conclusion: CG 筛选标记出的珠子组合可经由 juniper 转化为供原子级研究或合成使用的候选分子，从而打通了从高通量 CG 筛选回到具体化合物的通道。

Abstract: Transferable coarse-grained (CG) force fields compress chemical space: by aggregating atoms into a reduced set of interaction beads, models such as MARTINI reduce the number of distinguishable compounds by roughly three orders of magnitude, making high-throughput screening of thermodynamic properties tractable across soft matter, with drug--membrane permeability as a well-developed example. The compression is lossy and, so far, one-way: a screen returns a combination of beads, with no established route back to the compounds it stands for. Recovering those compounds--compositional backmapping--is a one-to-many inverse map, distinct from the better-studied conformational problem of rebuilding atomic coordinates from a known mapping. Here we formulate compositional backmapping as conditional graph generation by introducing juniper, a discrete denoising diffusion model over molecular graphs conditioned on the octanol--water partition free energy $ΔG_{\mathrm{W} \mapsto \mathrm{O}}$, the principal driver of MARTINI bead type assignment and hence a proxy for bead identity. Trained on molecules of up to 9 heavy atoms mapped onto one or two beads, juniper generates molecules that are 93\% valid and 92\% unique for two-bead targets, and whose $ΔG_{\mathrm{W} \mapsto \mathrm{O}}$ distributions track the target $ΔG^{\mathrm{CG}}_{\mathrm{W} \mapsto \mathrm{O}}$ linearly ($r^{2} \geq 0.96$), departing only in the hydrophobic and hydrophilic tails. Although the model receives no chemical information beyond a single scalar, the functional groups shift systematically with the imposed free energy, from branched hydrocarbons at the apolar end to amides, imides, and isocyanates at the polar end. A bead combination flagged by a CG screen can therefore be turned into candidate molecules for atomistic study or synthesis.

</details>


### [68] [Voltage-embedded equivariant machine learning potential for open system simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04696)
*Yiming Guan,Rongzhi Gao,ChiYung Yam,GuanHua Chen,Ziyang Hu*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种E(3)等变图神经网络（E(3)-equivariant graph neural network），通过嵌入电压偏压实现对开放系统（open quantum transport）电化学界面的机器学习势能建模，克服了传统方法只能处理闭系统均匀电场条件的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡条件下电化学界面的从头算建模成本高昂，而现有的机器学习势能大多仅针对均匀电场下的闭系统设计，无法胜任开放量子输运（open quantum transport）场景，亟需新的势能建模方法。

Method: 该方法将系统能量与力分解为偏压为零和偏压依赖两部分贡献，并以不同的向量编码（vector encodings）区分电极与散射区（scattering-region）原子，从而刻画非平衡条件；模型在有限的离散偏压数据上进行训练。

Result: 模型在有限训练数据下实现了高预测精度和稳健的外推迁移能力（extrapolation transferability）；应用于锂/水界面时，成功捕捉了电场诱导的水分子动态重取向，并复现了不同电极处的不对称电化学行为。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了E(3)等变图神经网络结合电压偏压嵌入范式在非平衡电化学界面模拟中的可行性，为开放系统能量技术的机器学习建模提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Modeling electrochemical interfaces under operational non-equilibrium conditions is vital for energy technologies but remains bottlenecked by the expensive cost of ab initio methods. Current machine learning potentials, largely designed for closed systems under homogeneous electric fields, are limited in open quantum transport applications. To overcome this, we present an E(3)-equivariant graph neural network that embeds voltage bias for open-system simulations. Our approach decouples the system energy and forces into zero-bias and bias-dependent contributions, assigning distinct vector encodings to electrode and scattering-region atoms to capture non-equilibrium conditions. Trained on limited discrete bias data, the model achieves high predictive accuracy and robust extrapolation transferability. When applied to a lithium/water interface, our model successfully captures the field-induced dynamic reorientation of water molecules and reproduces asymmetric electrochemical behavior at different electrodes.

</details>


### [69] [Operando imaging of intercalation memory in MXenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04977)
*Franz Groebmeyer,Mohsen Beladi,Christoph G. Gruber,Ruocun Wang,Pol Salles,Nhu Quynh Nguyen,Jakub Drnec,Yury Gogotsi,Emiliano Cortes*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过操作式干涉散射与干涉反射显微镜（operando interferometric scattering and interference reflection microscopy），在单层与多层Ti3C2Tx MXene薄片上解析质子驱动的插层动力学，发现即使仅叠加几层也会引入显著动力学势垒，并揭示了循环过程中形成的结构性记忆（structural memory）对后续插层行为的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ensemble测量会平均化不同厚度和堆叠方式的纳米片的插层路径，这在埃尺度（Angstrom-scale）受限条件下尤为严重。由于离子传输与溶剂重组及宿主变形耦合，插层动力学、可逆性与激活机制的起源尚未得到解析，需要通过单层尺度操作式观测来揭示。

Method: 结合操作式干涉散射与干涉反射显微镜，在单个单层和多层Ti3C2Tx MXene薄片上进行质子驱动的插层动力学测量；同时在电极尺度采用操作式同步辐射X射线衍射（operando synchrotron X-ray diffraction）来关联层间结构演化。

Result: 研究表明，即使少量层叠也会引入显著的动力学势垒。循环后纳米片分离为三种持续行为：可逆单层、恢复不完全且去插层迟缓的重堆叠多层，以及通过可逆折叠自稳定的相干堆叠多层。操作式X射线衍射在电极尺度证实了这种结构记忆，层间结构趋向于可复现的状态。

Conclusion: 研究发现循环诱导的结构性记忆掌控了层状MXene后续的插层行为，为理解插层动力学、可逆性与激活机制的起源提供了单层尺度的直接实验证据。

Abstract: Ion intercalation enables reversible control of charge, structure and function in layered solids. Yet layered materials contain nanosheets with different thicknesses and stacking, whose intercalation pathways are averaged out in ensemble measurements. This becomes critical under Angstrom-scale confinement, where ion transport couples to solvent reorganization and host deformation, leaving the origins of kinetics, reversibility and activation unresolved. Here we combine operando interferometric scattering and interference reflection microscopy to resolve proton-driven dynamics in individual single- and multilayer Ti3C2Tx MXene flakes and show that even a few stacked layers introduce a significant kinetic barrier. Cycling then separates nanosheets into three persistent behaviors: reversible monolayers, restacked multilayers with incomplete recovery and delayed deintercalation, and coherently stacked multilayers that self-stabilize through reversible folding. Operando synchrotron X-ray diffraction shows this memory at electrode scale, where the interlayer structure converges towards a reproducible state. Our findings reveal cycling-induced structural memory that governs subsequent intercalation in layered MXenes.

</details>

### [70] [Thermal boundaries for relativistic fluids without a conserved charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04925)
*Lorenzo Gavassino,Soeren Schlichting,Gabriel S. Denicol*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个无守恒粒子数的超相对论流体，其位于由两块平行导热板（保持固定且可能不同温度）围成的平板几何中。基于动理学理论将流体建模为自相互作用辐射（self-interacting radiation），墙与流体的相互作用由黑体定律控制，推导出流体动力学场的有效边界条件，证明墙表现为修正吸收边界而非恒温器，并证明相应的初边值问题是适定的。当两板温度不同时，系统发展出从热墙到冷墙的均匀稳态流动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解超相对论流体（ultrarelativistic fluid）与热传导壁面相互作用的微观基础，确定流体在有限几何中动力学的正确边界条件，并考察温度梯度下流体宏观行为的理论性质。

Method: 采用动理学理论（kinetic theory）方法建模，将流体视为自相互作用辐射：由墙体按黑体定律（black-body laws）发射和吸收的玻色子气体，粒子间的相互碰撞保证局部平衡。从这一微观设定出发推导流体动力学场（hydrodynamic fields）的有效边界条件，并研究相应初边值问题（initial-boundary-value problem）的适定性。

Result: 推导结果表明，墙体表现为修正的吸收边界（modified absorbing boundaries）而非恒温器（thermostats），流体在边界处的温度一般情况下不等于墙壁温度。证明了该初边值问题是适定的（well posed）。当两块板保持不同温度时，系统形成从热壁到冷壁的均匀稳态流动（uniform steady flow）；在小温差极限下，能量密度等于相应黑体能量密度的算术平均值。

Conclusion: 该研究从微观动理学设定出发，为超相对论流体在热传导壁面约束下的边界条件提供了自洽的第一性推导，揭示墙体并非理想的恒温热浴，并提出热驱动稳态流动的能量密度在小温差时遵循黑体能量密度的算术平均规律。

Abstract: We study an ultrarelativistic fluid with no conserved particle number in a planar slab geometry bounded by two parallel, thermally conducting plates held at fixed (possibly different) temperatures. The fluid-wall interaction is described within kinetic theory by modeling the fluid as self-interacting radiation: a gas of bosons emitted and absorbed by the walls according to black-body laws, while mutual collisions enforce local equilibration. From this microscopic setup we derive effective boundary conditions for the hydrodynamic fields, finding that the walls behave as modified absorbing boundaries (and not as thermostats). In particular, the fluid temperature at the boundary does not generally coincide with the wall temperature. We prove that the resulting initial-boundary-value problem is well posed. When the plates are held at different temperatures, the system develops a uniform steady flow from the hotter to the colder wall, with energy density equal to the arithmetic mean of the corresponding black-body energy densities in the limit of a small temperature difference.

</details>

### [71] [A Differentiable Neural Surrogate for Photon Propagation in Neutrino Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04695)
*Felix J. Yu,Berthy T. Feng,Nicholas Kamp,Carlos A. Argüelles*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文提出candela，一种基于可微SIREN神经场的高速光子传播模拟器，用于IceCube中微子天文台事件生成，比现有方法快50~100倍，且成本随中微子能量变化微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型中微子望远镜依赖切连科夫光（Cherenkov light）推断中微子特性，但模拟数十亿光子在高散射冰或水中的传播计算代价高昂，亟需更高效的事件生成方案。

Method: 引入candela——一种可微的SIREN神经场（neural field），学习IceCube探测器中光子的格林函数（Green's function）。对每个点状能量沉积和传感器，预测期望光子产额和完整到达时间分布；完整事件通过对带电粒子能量沉积分解为点源并叠加预测响应来生成。

Result: 基于蒙特卡洛（Monte-Carlo）模拟训练后，candela在生成速度上比现有方法快50至100倍，计算成本仅微弱依赖于中微子能量；中位产量与MC期望偏差保持在2%以内，在跨越六个光子计数数量级（decade）的范围内时间分布达到MC统计下限水平。

Conclusion: 该模型提供端到端（end-to-end）的、对事件参数的梯度，为优化散射介质性质（scattering-medium properties）开辟了路径——这类性质常常主导中微子望远镜中的系统不确定性（systematic uncertainties）。

Abstract: Large-volume neutrino telescopes infer neutrino properties from Cherenkov light, but simulating the transport of billions of photons through highly scattering ice or water is computationally costly. We introduce candela, a differentiable SIREN neural field that learns the photon Green's function of the IceCube Neutrino Observatory, a cubic-kilometer detector embedded in Antarctic glacial ice. Given a point-like energy deposit and sensor, it predicts the expected photon yield and full arrival-time distribution at the sensor. Complete events are simulated by decomposing charged-particle energy deposits into point-like sources and superposing their predicted sensor responses. Trained on Monte-Carlo simulations, candela generates events $50$--$100\times$ faster than existing methods, with cost scaling only weakly with neutrino energy. It keeps median yields within $2\%$ of the MC expectation and timing distributions at the MC statistical floor across six photon-count decades. The model also provides end-to-end gradients with respect to event parameters and opens a path toward optimizing scattering-medium properties, which often dominate systematic uncertainties in neutrino telescopes.

</details>


### [72] [Electromagnetic alignment and jet precession around supermassive black holes: Quasi-periodic oscillations in tidal disruption events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05350)
*Pau Amaro-Seoane,Leif Lui,Alejandro Torres-Orjuela,Xian Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用电磁角动量转移的协变表述，研究了潮汐瓦解事件（tidal disruption event）中的准周期振荡与喷流形成。通过分裂单极子磁场拓扑的小自旋展开，推导出电磁场对吸积等离子体施加的反作用力矩的解析闭合形式，证明该反作用力矩使内层迷你盘发生逆进动，并与顺行的Lense-Thirring进动耦合共同决定全局振荡频率。文章给出了可观测暂现信号的显式预测，并建立了从频率漂移时间导数提取黑洞自旋与磁通密度的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解潮汐瓦解事件中准周期振荡的产生机制以及吸积盘-磁场-喷流系统的物理耦合，是连接理论预言与多波段观测的核心课题。现有模型尚未从电磁反作用力角度统一解释迷你盘的进动动力学与喷流几何调制之间的关系。

Method: 采用广义相对论框架下的电磁角动量转移协变表述，结合Blandford-Znajek机制描述事件视界上的磁通量积累与相对论喷流的驱动；通过分裂单极子（split-monopole）磁场拓扑对力自由磁层（force-free magnetosphere）进行建模；对事件视界上的Noether电流密度执行小自旋展开，推导出电磁反作用力矩的闭合解析形式，并据此分析迷你盘的进动运动学与可观测信号。

Result: 电磁反作用力矩诱导迷你盘产生逆进动，与顺行的Lense-Thirring进动相耦合，共同决定准周期振荡的全局频率。预测了峰值-谷值通量比随盘对齐的单调衰减，以及表现为先增长后渐近缩短的爆发间隔频率漂移特征。磁通量耗竭会停滞对齐过程，在晚期产生恒定的残余调制振幅。建立了可直接从频率漂移时间导数提取黑洞自旋和磁通密度的方法。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了电磁反作用驱动迷你盘逆进动的解析机制，并将潮汐瓦解事件的准周期振荡与其多波段可观测特征（X射线与射电通量调制）统一在一个自洽的物理框架内。所提出的参数提取方法独立于光谱连续谱拟合，为利用时间域信号约束黑洞性质提供了新的观测途径。

Abstract: We evaluate quasi-periodic oscillations and jet formation in tidal disruption events using the covariant formulation of electromagnetic angular-momentum transfer. General-relativistic frame-dragging tears apart misaligned transient accretion flows, forming an isolated inner mini-disk. The accumulation of magnetic flux on the event horizon powers a relativistic jet via the Blandford-Znajek mechanism. Because the magnetic field anchors to the precessing mini-disk, the jet axis rotates, generating geometric modulations in the observed X-ray and radio fluxes. To ensure physical consistency with the force-free magnetosphere required to launch a Blandford-Znajek jet, we model the electromagnetic back-reaction using a split-monopole magnetic field topology. By performing a small-spin expansion of the Noether current density over the event horizon, we derive a closed-form analytical reaction torque exerted by the electromagnetic field on the accretion plasma. We evaluate the resulting kinematics to show that the electromagnetic back-reaction induces a retrograde precession of the mini-disk, coupling with the prograde Lense-Thirring precession to dictate the global oscillation frequency. We formulate explicit predictions for observable transient signals and predict a monotonic attenuation of the peak-to-trough flux ratio as the mini-disk aligns, as well as a specific frequency drift signature characterized by an initial lengthening followed by an asymptotic shortening of the time interval between consecutive flares. We establish an analytical mechanism where magnetic flux depletion stalls alignment, predicting a constant residual modulation amplitude at late times. We formulate a methodology to extract the black hole spin and the magnetic flux density directly from the temporal derivatives of this predicted frequency drift, operating independently of spectral continuum fitting.

</details>

*Pradeeptha R Jain*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了非齐次泊松陷阱环境中随机游走的退火（annealed）存活概率，在d=1,2维下确定了其渐近行为，并将陷阱环境在d≥1下的大数定律、中心极限定理和大偏差原理从齐次情形推广至非齐次情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的相关工作仅针对齐次（homogeneous）泊松陷阱环境推导了存活概率的渐近行为，本文旨在将这一理论框架拓展至初始陷阱构型具有空间不均匀性的非齐次情形，探讨环境的非齐次性如何影响存活概率的渐近衰减。

Method: 研究对象为\mathbb{Z}^d上移动陷阱构成的非齐次泊松环境中的随机游走，通过在d=1,2维下对平均陷阱强度施加适当假设，分析退火存活概率的渐近行为；同时在d≥1维下证明了非齐次泊松陷阱环境的大数定律（law of large numbers）、中心极限定理（central limit theorem）和大偏差原理（large deviation principle）。

Result: 在d=1,2维下确定了非齐次陷阱环境中随机游走的退火存活概率的渐近形式，并阐明了初始陷阱构型的不均匀性对这些渐近结果的影响；该结果推广了齐次情形（参见DGRS2012的结论）。在d≥1维下建立的非齐次陷阱环境的极限定理推广了齐次情形中的相应结论。此外还给出了一类非齐次陷阱环境的具体实例，其中存活概率的衰减率可被精确识别，同时也展示了存活概率随时间不衰减的例子。

Conclusion: 研究表明，陷阱环境的空间非齐次性在低维情形下会实质性地改变随机游走存活概率的渐近行为，且不同非齐次构型可能导致截然不同的存活行为（从明确可识别的指数衰减到完全不衰减）。

Abstract: We study the annealed survival probability of a random walk in an inhomogeneous Poisson environment of mobile traps on $\mathbb{Z}^d$. In dimensions $d=1,2$, we determine the asymptotics for the annealed survival probability of the walker under suitable assumptions on the average trap intensity and study how inhomogeneity in the initial trap configuration affects these asymptotics. Our results extend the asymptotics proved for the homogeneous setting in \cite{DGRS2012}. We also establish a law of large numbers, central limit theorem and large deviation principle for the inhomogeneous Poisson trap environment in $d\ge 1$, generalising the corresponding results proved for the homogeneous case considered in \cite{CG1984}. We present a class of examples of inhomogeneous trap environments where the decay rate of survival probability can be identified and also instances where it does not decay with time.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Kenyon和Okounkov（2007年）关于随机菱形铺砌中高度函数涨落收敛到高斯自由场的预测，为该问题提供严格的数学证明。

Method: 引入一个铺砌作用函数（tiling action function），通过其临界点的性质编码极限形状的几何结构：液态区域中该函数有一对复共轭临界点，北极边界上具有重复的实临界点，冻结区域中则具有互异的实临界点。利用该作用函数，以显式的单围道和双围道积分构造逆Kasteleyn矩阵的近似，并证明该近似在整个多边形域上一致成立。

Result: 在液态区域连通且边界数据不在任何内部点强制高度的条件下，证明了中心化高度函数在液态区域中的涨落收敛到高斯自由场。

Conclusion: 高斯自由场的收敛性由标准的核计算直接推得，为随机菱形铺砌模型中的涨落行为提供了统一的分析框架。

Abstract: We establish Gaussian free field fluctuations for uniformly random lozenge tilings of simply connected polygonal domains with $3d$ sides whose directions cycle through the three lattice directions. More precisely, assuming that the liquid region is connected and that the boundary data do not force the height at any interior point, we prove that the fluctuations of centered height function converge to the Gaussian free field in the liquid region, confirming a prediction of Kenyon and Okounkov from 2007.
  We introduce a tiling action function that encodes the geometry of the limit shape through its critical points. The action function has a complex conjugate pair of critical points in the liquid region, repeated real critical points on the arctic boundary, and distinct real critical points in the frozen region. Using this tiling action function, we construct an approximation to the inverse Kasteleyn matrix in terms of explicit single-contour and double-contour integrals and prove that the approximation is uniform throughout the polygonal domain. The convergence to the Gaussian free field then follows from standard kernel computations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者发展用于双曲材料与拓扑绝缘体界面的表面电磁波解析判据，涵盖色散关系推导、场穿透深度与带隙分析。在无损耗模型中分析介电对比度与体拓扑耦合的影响；对有损耗的钛—硅双曲有效介质（hyperbolic effective medium）与硒化铋界面，采用增强界面响应以便揭示复数分支结构，并检验了导波模式的四个物理条件。

Result: 结果表明，无损耗情形下带边主要由介电对比度控制，体拓扑耦合仅产生较小频移与弱色散修正；六方氮化硼与硒化铋组合时，置于低介电衬底上的薄拓扑绝缘体薄膜可显著拓宽传播窗口。有损耗情形下，只有当场从界面衰减、沿传播方向一致衰减、穿透深度有限且能流剖面一致时，色散关系的根才对应物理导波模式；表观带隙与跳变可能源于横向衰减量接近平方根支割线。

Conclusion: 这些结果为中红外光子结构中调控色散、场束缚、带隙与传播损耗提供了设计指南；有损耗计算为示意性而非定量预测，后续需结合更定量模型进行验证。

Abstract: We develop analytical criteria for surface electromagnetic waves at interfaces between hyperbolic materials and topological insulators. The analysis identifies propagation conditions, field penetration depths, and spectral band gaps. In the lossless model, the band edge is governed primarily by dielectric contrast, while the bulk topological coupling produces only a small shift and a weak correction to the dispersion. For structures combining hexagonal boron nitride with bismuth selenide, a thin topological insulator film on a dielectric substrate with low permittivity can substantially broaden the accessible propagation window. We also analyze a dissipative titanium and silicon hyperbolic effective medium interfaced with bismuth selenide. To make the complex branch structure visible, this lossy calculation uses an intentionally enhanced interface response and is therefore illustrative rather than a quantitative prediction for a conventional topological insulator. In the lossy case, a root of the dispersion relation represents a physical guided mode only when the fields decay away from the interface, attenuate consistently along the propagation direction, have finite penetration depths, and exhibit a consistent energy flow profile. Apparent gaps and jumps can occur when the complex quantities controlling transverse decay approach square root branch cuts. These results provide design guidelines for controlling dispersion, confinement, band gaps, and propagation loss in mid-infrared photonic structures.

</details>

### [76] [Strong-Drive Floquet Engineering of Interacting Qudits: From Finite-Duration Controls to Emergent Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04309)
*Ryan Scott,V. W. Scarola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种强驱动 Floquet 理论，将实验上可实现的有限脉宽脉冲波形直接纳入有效相互作用的设计中，使脉冲时长、振幅和形状成为可控参数而非误差来源，并在相互作用三能级系统中展示了有限时长驱动在创建新相互作用和改变系统对称性方面的独特能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数 Floquet 驱动方案假设理想的瞬时脉冲，而实验中不可避免地使用有限时长和有限形状的脉冲；这种失配在相互作用的多能级系统（qudit）中尤为突出，因为随能级数增加，可能的控制方式迅速增多。

Method: 开发了一种强驱动 Floquet 理论，将有限时长脉冲波形直接纳入有效相互作用的推导设计中；随后对相互作用三能级系统进行数值验证，分别测试短时演化和多体动力学。

Result: 单脉冲可将对角相互作用转化为以向列相互作用（nematic interaction）为主的量子自旋-1 模型；受超冷极性分子实验启发的脉冲方案可产生具有 SU(2)×U(1) 和 SU(3) 扩展对称性的模型；数值测试确认了理论描述的准确性。

Conclusion: 有限时长驱动使多能级系统能够实现量子比特（qubit）系统所不具备的新能力，包括创建原系统中不存在的新相互作用和显著改变其对称性；该理论为设计相互作用 qudit 平台的有限时长控制提供了一个可扩展的分析框架。

Abstract: Floquet driving uses periodic controls to tailor the behavior of quantum systems, with applications in quantum analogue simulation, sensing, and the protection of quantum information. Most approaches are designed using idealized, instantaneous pulses, even though experiments necessarily use pulses with finite duration and shape. This mismatch becomes especially challenging for interacting $d$-level systems, or qudits, because the number of possible controls grows rapidly with the number of levels. We develop a strong-drive Floquet theory that incorporates experimentally realizable pulse waveforms directly into the design of the effective interactions. The pulse duration, amplitude, and shape therefore become useful control parameters rather than sources of error. We show that systems with more than two levels offer capabilities unavailable in qubit systems: finite-duration driving can create new interactions that are absent from the original system and can substantially change its symmetries. We demonstrate these capabilities for interacting three-level systems. A single pulse transforms a diagonal interaction into a quantum spin-1 model dominated by nematic interactions, while pulse protocols motivated by trapped ultracold polar molecules produce models with enlarged $SU(2)\times U(1)$ and $SU(3)$ symmetries. Numerical tests of both short-time evolution and many-body dynamics confirm the accuracy of the resulting description. Our results provide a scalable analytical framework for designing finite-duration controls in interacting qudit platforms.

</details>

*P. Zahalka,S. Huijser,A. Pancaldi,S. Kruger,X. Zhao,Z. Liu,I. Suleiman,R. Jensen,L. Stefan,A. Ludwig,V. Remesh,J. C. Loredo,P. Lodahl*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进的光学量子技术（advanced optical quantum technologies）要求在非常高的速率下产生高质量的量子光（quantum light generation），这是推动本工作的核心动因。

Method: 该研究构建了一种确定性单光子源，通过同时结合高激发速率（high excitation rates）与高系统效率（high system efficiency）来实现高单光子通量。

Result: 该单光子源实现了超过500 MHz的光纤内单光子通量，并输出超过100 pW的光功率，该功率可通过现成的商用功率计（off-the-shelf powermeter）直接测量。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种简单直接的方式来确定单光子源的光纤效率（source fiber efficiency），有望推动先进光学量子技术的实际应用。

Abstract: Advanced optical quantum technologies demands high quality quantum light generation at very high rates. Here, we report on a deterministic single-photon source that simultaneously combines high excitation rates with high system efficiency to reach over 500 MHz of in-fibre single-photon flux. The source delivers optical power of over 100 pW, as is measured with an off-the-shelf powermeter, enabling a simple and direct way of determining the single-photon source fiber efficiency.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者提出一种基于物理的数据同化框架：粒子追踪测速提供液相速度场，热学和运动学边界条件约束逆问题；该框架将液相水中的对流-扩散（advection-diffusion）方程与冰中的热传导（conduction）方程耦合，用于同时重构平均温度场并量化跨水-冰界面的热输运，且保持微创性与自由表面系统兼容性。

Result: 该方法在实验室实验中成功演示，实验系统中液相水与冰接触的温度范围驱动了cabbeling诱导的对流，证明了该框架能够重构温度场并分析跨冰-水界面的热交换与液相能量学。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究低温圈（cryospheric）水生系统中的耦合热流体动力学提供了新手段，可应用于冰-水界面热交换与液相能量分析，并可推广至食品与能源工业中的相变过程研究。

Abstract: What controls heat transport when liquid water interacts with ice? Answering this question is essential for understanding water bodies undergoing phase change. Yet experimental progress remains limited by the lack of a minimally invasive methodology for simultaneously resolving temperature fields in coupled liquid water and non-isothermal ice systems. Here, we introduce a physics-based data-assimilation method for reconstructing temperature fields in buoyancy-driven flows interacting with non-isothermal ice. Particle tracking velocimetry provides the liquid velocity field, while thermal and kinematic boundary conditions constrain the inverse problem. The method couples advection--diffusion in liquid water with conduction in ice to reconstruct simultaneous mean temperature fields and quantify heat transport across the water--ice interface, while remaining minimally invasive and compatible with free-surface systems. We demonstrate the method in laboratory experiments in which the temperature range across the liquid water in contact with ice drives cabbeling-induced convection. This framework enables investigation of coupled thermo-fluid dynamics in cryospheric aquatic systems, including heat exchange at the ice-water interface and liquid-phase energetics, with broader applications to phase-change processes in food and energy industries.

</details>


### [79] [Elastic turbulence in straight confined geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04617)
*Giulio Foggi Rota,Daulet Izbassarov,Aditya Tekriwal,Marco Edoardo Rosti*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文报道了在直管道（ducts）和圆管（pipes）两种受限几何结构中均观察到持续的非线性混沌动力学行为，证明其与理想化构型中识别的弹性湍流（elastic turbulence, ET）状态一致，从而确立ET为几何无关的吸引子（attractor），并指出圆管中存在独特的转变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性湍流在直线几何中的研究主要集中在理想化构型（如周期性剪切和平面槽道流），但实验通常受限于密闭系统（如管道和圆管）。尽管在管道中观察到混沌弹性涨落，其在圆管中的起始行为因几何约束而存在争议，需要澄清两种受限几何中观察到的是否为同一ET状态。

Method: 在直线几何（ducts和pipes）中进行实验观测，捕获两种几何中的混沌弹性涨落，并将其动力学行为与理想化构型中已识别的ET状态进行对比分析。

Result: 在管道和圆管两种几何中均观测到持续的非线性混沌动力学，且两者对应于理想化构型中识别的同一ET状态，表明ET是几何无关的动力学吸引子。

Conclusion: 研究确立了弹性湍流作为几何无关吸引子的普遍性，同时揭示圆管中存在区别于其他几何的独特的转变机制（transition mechanism）。

Abstract: Elastic turbulence (ET) in straight geometries has been extensively studied in idealised configurations such as periodic shear and planar channel flow, yet experiments are typically restricted to confined systems like ducts and pipes. While chaotic elastic fluctuations are observed in ducts, their onset in pipes is debated due to geometric constraints. Here we report sustained chaotic dynamics in both geometries and show they correspond to the same ET state identified in idealised setups. Our results establish ET as a geometry-independent attractor and point to a distinct transition mechanism in pipes.

</details>

### [80] [Spin-dependent azimuthal asymmetry of coherent J/$ψ$ photoproduction in hadronic PbPb collisions at $\sqrt{s_\mathrm{NN}}$ = 5.36 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04376)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 该论文报告了CMS实验在铅-铅（PbPb）对撞中首次以超过5倍标准差显著度观测到相干光生J/ψ介子衰变μ子相对于二阶事件平面的方位角不对称性，测得振幅A2 = −0.194 ± 0.022（统计）± 0.018（系统），实验数据与相干光生J/ψ介子继承入射光子线性极化的理论模型一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究相干光生（coherent photoproduction）矢量介子衰变产物的自旋相关方位角不对称性，以理解光生过程的极化机制，并探索利用该不对称性进行反应平面（reaction plane）测定的新方法。

Method: 基于CMS探测器在大型强子对撞机（LHC）上以核子-核子质心能量5.36 TeV收集的强子铅-铅对撞数据（积分亮度1.26 nb⁻¹），测量相干光生J/ψ介子衰变μ子的方位角分布，以衰变μ子方位角分布的二阶谐振振幅A2量化其相对二阶事件平面（event plane）的不对称性。

Result: 首次观测到负的A2 = −0.194 ± 0.022（统计）± 0.018（系统），显著度超过五个标准差；A2值对J/ψ介子快度（rapidity）及碰撞离子重叠程度无显著依赖。数据与相干光生J/ψ介子继承入射光子线性极化的理论模型符合良好。

Conclusion: 该结果与超外围碰撞（ultraperipheral collisions）中相干ρ⁰→π⁺π⁻光生的近期测量共同提供了对相干光生矢量介子衰变产物方位角不对称性中自旋依赖性的直接观测；此外，观测到的与事件平面的关联可能为未来反应平面的确定提供新途径。

Abstract: We report a measurement of the azimuthal asymmetry of muons from the decay of coherently photoproduced J/$ψ$ mesons with respect to the second-order event plane in hadronic lead-lead (PbPb) collisions. The analysis is based on data collected by the CMS experiment at the LHC at a nucleon-nucleon center-of-mass energy of 5.36 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 1.26 nb$^{-1}$. The asymmetry is quantified using the amplitude $A_{2}$ of the second-order harmonic modulation of the decay-muon azimuthal distribution. A negative $A_{2}$ = $-$0.194 $\pm$ 0.022 (stat.) $\pm$ 0.018 (syst.) is observed with a significance greater than five standard deviations for the first time. The $A_{2}$ values show no significant dependence on the J/$ψ$ meson rapidity or the degree of overlap of the colliding ions. A theoretical model in which the coherently photoproduced J/$ψ$ meson inherits the linear polarization of the incident photon is consistent with the data. When considered together with recent measurements of coherent $ρ^0$ $\to$ $π^+π^-$ photoproduction in ultraperipheral collisions, these results provide a direct observation of spin dependence in the azimuthal asymmetry of decay products from coherently photoproduced vector mesons. In addition, the observed correlation with the event plane may enable a novel approach to reaction plane determination in the future.

</details>
