### [81] [Fukui-Kawakami chains: spectrum and hidden $\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$-symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04378)
*Rob Klabbers,Antoine Lefebvre*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了由Fukui和Kawakami引入的扭曲边界条件下的Haldane–Shastry（HS）自旋链（即Fukui–Kawakami（FK）链），通过其与未扭曲HS链的直接联系分析谱随扭曲参数的变化，解释了部分谱可由扭曲Bethe方程描述的原因，并发现反周期边界条件下该链等价于近期引入的'最小极化'长程模型，从而揭示其隐藏的$\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fukui和Kawakami已证明可通过引入扭曲边界条件来变形三角HS自旋链，但此前对这种扭曲HS链（FK链）的性质尚缺乏系统理解。本文旨在重新审视这类FK链，深入分析其谱结构与扭曲参数的依赖关系。

Method: 利用FK链与未扭曲的HS链之间的直接联系来分析谱，揭示部分谱可由Fukui–Kawakami扭曲Bethe方程描述并由Yangian最高权态构造，这些态由组合学数据'motif'标记。通过对Bethe方程无法描述的态的能谱分析，以及反周期边界条件下的特殊情形，进一步研究其隐藏对称性。

Result: 部分谱可由Fukui–Kawakami扭曲Bethe方程描述并源于Yangian最高权态（由motif标记），但仍存在其他不遵循这些Bethe方程的descendant态，其能量可表示为两个单粒子能量之和。反周期边界条件下该链等价于Basu-Mallick等人引入的'最小极化'长程模型，并具有隐藏的$\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$对称性，可将HS motifs与相应的超对称$\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$motifs联系起来。

Conclusion: FK链谱中存在不服从扭曲Bethe方程的descendant态，其能量行为暗示存在额外的隐藏对称性。反周期情形下与'最小极化'长程模型的等价性揭示了$\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$超对称结构，建立了HS motifs与超对称motifs之间的对应关系。

Abstract: Fukui and Kawakami showed that the trigonometric Haldane--Shastry (HS) spin chain can be deformed by introducing twisted boundary conditions. In this work we revisit the resulting twisted HS chains, which we call Fukui--Kawakami (FK) chains.
  We analyse their spectra in dependence of the twist parameter, utilising a direct connection with the (untwisted) HS chain. This allows us to explain why part of the spectrum can be described by Fukui and Kawakami's twisted Bethe equations, and can be constructed from Yangian highest weight states. These states can be labelled by combinatorial data called `motifs', as in the HS chain, which cover some of the (deformed) descendants. We furthermore show that there are other descendants which do not follow from these Bethe equations, but whose energy can be described as a sum of two single-particle energies, suggesting additional hidden symmetries.
  We then focus on the special case of antiperiodic boundary conditions, and show that this chain coincides with the `minimally polarised' long-range model recently introduced by Basu-Mallick, Finkel, and González-López. Remarkably, this connection implies that the antiperiodic chain has a hidden $\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$-symmetry, which we use to relate the HS motifs to their `supersymmetric' $\mathfrak{gl}(1|1)$ counterparts.

</details>


### [82] [Scaling laws and energy dissipation in the dynamics of SLE curve as a 1D turbulence model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04719)
*Yusuke K. Shibasaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文论证了随机Loewner演化（SLE）可作为一维（1D）湍流模型：通过时间坐标变换获得满足Richardson定律的扩散过程，并推导出差分参数κ的条件使其满足Kolmogorov 4/5定律，数值模拟验证了标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索随机Loewner演化（SLE）作为一维湍流（turbulence）模型的可行性，为湍流研究提供新的数学工具与理论框架。

Method: 首先对SLE进行时间坐标变换，构造满足Richardson定律的时变扩散过程；随后解析推导使该扩散过程满足Kolmogorov 4/5定律所需的差分参数κ条件，并将其转化为Loewner熵条件的约束；最后通过数值模拟验证一维湍流模型的标度关系。

Result: 推导出使扩散过程满足Kolmogorov 4/5定律的差分参数κ条件可表示为对Loewner熵Ｓ_Loew的条件约束，其中包含Loewner驱动函数中的白高斯噪声项W_s；数值模拟验证了该一维湍流模型的标度关系。

Conclusion: 通过引入适当的时间坐标变换并施加对κ和Loewner熵的条件，标准SLE可以成为一维湍流的有益模型。

Abstract: In this study, we demonstrate the characteristics of the stochastic Loewner evolution (SLE) as a one-dimensional (1D) turbulence model. First, we show that the diffusion process $x(t)$ obtained by the time coordinate change in the SLE becomes the time dependent diffusion process that satisfies Richardson's law of turbulence.Subsequently, we derive the condition on the diffusivity parameter $κ$ such that the diffusion process $x(t)$ satisfies Kolmogorov's 4/5th law, which determines the relation between the mean energy dissipation and velocity in the particles of turbulence. Further mathematical analysis showed that this condition is seen as the condition on Loewner entropy $S_{\mathrm{Loew}}$, which includes the white Gaussian noise term $W_{s}$ in the Loewner driving function of the present model. In addition, the numerical simulations were performed to verify the scaling relations of this 1D turbulence model. These results suggest that the standard SLE becomes a beneficial model of 1D turbulence by introducing an appropriate time coordinate change and imposing the conditions on $κ$ and $S_{\mathrm{Loew}}$.

</details>


### [83] [Diffusion under competing bulk and surface stopping mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05247)
*Yilin Ye*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究有界区域中反射扩散（reflected diffusion）在两种竞争停止机制下的统计性质：速率为 p 的体衰减和由边界局部时（boundary local time）超过指数阈值的表面反应（surface reaction，速率 q）。研究导出了停止时间 T 与边界局部时 L 的边际分布、联合拉普拉斯变换以及混合矩，并确立了 p⟨T⟩+q⟨L⟩=1 及累积风险 pT+qL 服从单位速率指数分布等关键恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解反射扩散在体衰减与表面反应两种独立竞争机制下的停止行为，统一刻画竞争马尔可夫过程的统计学规律，为相关扩散问题的理论工具与概率定律提供支撑。

Method: 以表面反应先于体衰减发生的分裂概率（splitting probability）φ 为中心参量，推导 T 与 L 的边际分布、联合拉普拉斯变换（joint Laplace transform）及完整混合矩层级；借助 Robin-拉普拉斯算子（Robin-Laplacian）与广义 Steklov 谱（generalized Steklov spectra）给出 φ 的等价表示；对三维球体进行显式计算，并利用蒙特卡洛模拟验证累积风险律。

Result: 建立了恒等式 p⟨T⟩+q⟨L⟩=1，并证明累积风险 pT+qL 服从参数为1的指数分布（exponential distribution）；对三维球情形给出了竞争速率 p、q 如何控制 φ 及 (T,L) 统计量的显式结果；蒙特卡洛模拟验证了累积风险律的普适性。

Conclusion: 两个竞争停止机制的竞争结构可由分裂概率 φ 统一刻画，累积风险服从单位速率指数分布构成一种普适规律，该结果对体衰减与表面反应的竞争扩散问题提供了完整的概率统计分析框架。

Abstract: We investigate reflected diffusion in a bounded domain subject to two independent, competing stopping mechanisms: an exponentially distributed bulk lifetime of rate $p$ and a surface reaction triggered when the boundary local time exceeds an independent exponential threshold of rate $q$. Denoting by $T$ the stopping time and by $L$ the acquired boundary local time at stopping, we derive their marginal distributions, joint Laplace transform, and complete hierarchy of mixed moments. These statistics are determined by the splitting probability $φ$ that surface reaction occurs before bulk decay. In particular, we establish the identity $p\expect{T}+q\expect{L}=1$ and show that the cumulative risk $pT+qL$ is exponentially distributed with unit rate. We further obtain equivalent representations of $φ$ in terms of the Robin-Laplacian and the generalized Steklov spectra. Explicit results for a three-dimensional ball reveal how competing rates $p,q$ control $φ$ and the $(T,L)$ statistics. Monte Carlo simulations test the universal cumulative-risk law.

</details>


### [84] [Breakdown of the Overdamped Approximation in Fluctuating Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04668)
*Kazuki Fukutani,Takuma Akimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了在摩擦系数随时间涨落的环境中，过阻尼（overdamped）与欠阻尼（underdamped）Langevin动力学在长时间输运行为上的差异，证明环境平均（environmental averaging）与惯性消除（inertial elimination）在时间异质环境中不具有可交换性，并建立了过阻尼近似有效性的时间尺度判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过阻尼近似广泛应用于复杂环境中的布朗运动描述，但当摩擦系数本身随时间涨落时，其有效性变得非平凡。作者旨在揭示当环境涨落较快于速度弛豫时，环境平均与惯性消除是否导致不同的长时间输运行为，以及过阻尼近似在时间异质环境中的适用范围。

Method: 通过比较在相同涨落摩擦并满足共同温度下涨落-耗散关系（fluctuation-dissipation relation）的欠阻尼与过阻尼Langevin动力学，分析了两种描述在长时间极限下的有效扩散系数。对快涨落摩擦情形给出解析结果；对两态马尔可夫（two-state Markov）摩擦过程精确推导了有限时间有效扩散系数并识别完整交叉区间；对一般平稳摩擦过程建立快涨落结果，并通过连续对数Ornstein–Uhlenbeck环境验证，同时独立确定特征交叉时间。

Result: 对于快速涨落的摩擦，欠阻尼动力学的有效扩散系数为 $D_{\rm eff}^{\rm under}=k_{\rm B}T/\langle\gamma\rangle$（由算术平均摩擦决定），而过阻尼动力学给出 $D_{\rm eff}^{\rm over}=k_{\rm B}T\langle\gamma^{-1}\rangle$（由调和平均决定）——两者一般不同。两态马尔可夫情形下可精确描述由速度弛豫与环境切换竞争控制的完整交叉；快速涨落结果对所有平稳摩擦过程成立，并通过对数Ornstein–Uhlenbeck连续环境得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了环境平均与惯性消除之间的非可交换性（noncommutativity），建立了时间异质环境中过阻尼动力学有效性的依赖于时间尺度的判据，并指出快速的环境涨落可增强而非抑制惯性的影响。

Abstract: The overdamped approximation is widely used to describe Brownian motion in complex environments, but its validity becomes nontrivial when the friction coefficient itself fluctuates in time. We investigate this problem by comparing underdamped and overdamped Langevin dynamics subject to the same fluctuating friction and satisfying the fluctuation--dissipation relation at a common temperature. We show that environmental averaging and inertial elimination generally lead to different long-time transport when environmental fluctuations are fast compared with velocity relaxation. For rapidly fluctuating friction, the underdamped dynamics is governed by the arithmetic mean friction and yields $D_{\rm eff}^{\rm under}=k_{\rm B}T/\langleγ\rangle$, whereas the overdamped dynamics gives $D_{\rm eff}^{\rm over}=k_{\rm B}T\langleγ^{-1}\rangle$. For a two-state Markov friction, we derive the finite-time effective diffusion coefficient exactly and identify the full crossover between these two regimes, controlled by the competition between velocity relaxation and environmental switching. We further establish the fast-fluctuation result for general stationary friction processes and verify it for a continuous log-Ornstein--Uhlenbeck environment, for which the characteristic crossover time can also be determined independently. Our results reveal a noncommutativity between environmental averaging and inertial elimination, establish a timescale-dependent criterion for the validity of overdamped dynamics in temporally heterogeneous environments, and show that rapid environmental fluctuations can enhance, rather than suppress, the consequences of inertia.

</details>


### [85] [Mean-field theory for quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04717)
*Mylène Martirosyan,Astrid Monin-Baroille,Leïla Moueddene,Mohammed M. Shabat,Bertrand Berche*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文是一篇教学性短论文，旨在探讨均场理论（mean-field theory）在量子相变（quantum phase transitions）这一较少被涉足的领域中如何应用，并以伊辛模型（Ising model）和布卢姆-卡佩尔模型（Blume-Capel model）作为具体研究对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均场近似在处理经典相变时非常有效，但在零温量子相变中的应用相对少见；作者希望澄清均场方法在该领域中的适用方式，填补学术教学上的空白。

Method: 以教学性讨论的方式，将均场理论这一经典方法推广至量子相变情境，选取伊辛模型和布卢姆-卡佩尔模型为具体分析对象。

Result: 摘要中未报告具体数值或定量结果。

Conclusion: 作者旨在阐明均场方法在量子相变这一相对陌生领域中的使用方式，从而扩展该经典近似理论的应用视野。

Abstract: Mean-field theory is a widely used approximation for describing phase transitions, particularly effective above the upper critical dimension. Its origins can be traced back to the van der Waals theory of the liquid-gas transition and Weiss's molecular field theory of the paramagnetic-ferromagnetic transition. However, it was Lev D. Landau who provided a unifying and general framework applicable to a broad class of physical systems. The mean-field approach typically involves neglecting thermal fluctuations, which is a reasonable assumption in many classical contexts. However, its application to quantum phase transitions at zero temperature is less common. The aim of this short pedagogical paper is to explore and clarify the use of the mean-field approach in the less familiar domain of quantum phase transitions. We specifically consider the Ising model and the Blume-Capel model.

</details>


### [86] [Thermodynamic learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04732)
*F. Corberi,S. dello Russo,G. Messuti,S. Scarpetta,L. Smaldone*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文探讨了训练热力学系统（thermodynamic system）执行记忆与泛化任务的可行性。训练通过施加外部场（external fields）实现，不引入任何逻辑或算法规则，与常规机器学习截然不同。该方法原则上可进行精确解析计算，并在退火二值耦合伊辛系统（Ising system with annealed dichotomous couplings）上验证，结果显示小系统即具备优异记忆和良好泛化能力，且性能随系统规模增大而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习依赖算法规则和逻辑框架进行学习，而作者希望探索一种全新的范式：仅通过麦克罗变量（micro-variables）由哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）和统计力学规则完全确定的热力学系统来执行学习任务，从而检验物理系统本身是否具备记忆和泛化能力，避免外部规则的介入。

Method: 系统训练通过施加合适的外部场（external fields）完成，外部场扮演数据（data）角色。该方法不引入任何额外的逻辑或算法规则，仅依赖统计力学框架，且原则上可进行精确解析计算。作者将这一通用方法专门化到具有退火二值耦合（annealed dichotomous couplings）的原型伊辛系统上，研究其学习能力。

Result: 结果显示，即使在小规模系统中，该系统也表现出优异的记忆（memorization）能力和良好的泛化（generalization）能力，且这两项能力有随系统尺寸增大而改善的趋势。

Conclusion: 纯热力学系统仅通过外部场驱动，无需算法规则即可实现记忆和泛化任务，表明物理计算（physical computing）可作为机器学习的一种替代性基础框架，并具备随规模扩展的潜力。

Abstract: We discuss the possibility to train a thermodynamic system, whose micro-variables are fully determined through the Hamiltonian by the usual statistical mechanical rules, to perform tasks such as memorization and generalization. Training is achieved by the application of suitable external fields, playing the role of {\it data}. At variance with conventional machine learning, no other logical or algorithmic rules are introduced. The system is amenable, in principle, to exact analytical calculations. We specialize this general approach to a prototypical Ising system with annealed dichotomous couplings and study its learning ability. Results indicate excellent memorization and good generalization capacity already for small systems, and a tendency to improve with system size.

</details>


### [87] [Universal Thermodynamic Law Governing Stochastic Pendulum Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04957)
*Yuki Izumida*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究表明，对于一类以弱非线性振子描述的随机摆钟，其相位不确定性与熵产生之积仅取决于非线性度，呈现通用且简洁的形式，超越了传统热力学不确定关系（TUR）的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摆钟作为物理器件，其精度从根本上受热力学定律限制。然而，既有热力学不确定关系（thermodynamic uncertainty relation, TUR）针对欠阻尼系统（如随机摆钟）会被违反，因此需要新的理论框架来描述这类系统的精度上限。

Method: 将随机摆钟建模为一类弱非线性振子，推导其相位不确定性与熵产生之积的通用表达式，并通过多个代表性摆钟模型检验该关系式的有效性与适用范围。

Result: 发现该不确定关系乘积仅取决于系统的非线性度（degree of nonlinearity），形式简单且具有普适性，并通过多个代表性模型验证了其有效性与边界。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了超越传统TUR的、支配随机摆钟的普适热力学原理，为设计在随机环境中高效运行的优化摆钟提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Clocks' performance, especially their precision, is fundamentally limited by thermodynamic laws as they are physical devices. Yet, it is known that the established thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR), which imposes an upper bound on the uncertainty product of the precision of oscillations and entropy production, is violated for underdamped systems such as stochastic pendulum clocks. Here, we show that for a general class of stochastic pendulum clocks described as a weakly nonlinear oscillator the uncertainty product of the phase of a pendulum clock and entropy production takes a universal and simple form that depends solely on the degree of nonlinearity. Its validity and limitations are examined using several representative models of pendulum clocks. Our framework reveals a universal thermodynamic principle governing stochastic pendulum clocks beyond the conventional TUR and provides a foundation for designing optimal pendulum clocks that operate efficiently in stochastic environments.

</details>

Abstract: We suggest a model of a diffusive process inside a fractal sponge structure, which is a generalization of the diffusion processes on a comb and fractal mesh structure. Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将张量网络（tensor network）方法应用于场耦合XY模型，基于日收益率统计设置资产特征场和相关耦合，通过相关性距离、四点Gromov双曲性和Ward聚类构建稀疏交互路径，并以傅里叶-贝塞尔展开将连续角向配分函数映射为有限电流张量网络，从而获得资产配置权重。研究在五个股票市场、连续逆温度β和浓度γ范围内验证了该方法与长期多Markowitz前沿及标准基准的可比性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种从金融时间序列出发、能够自然引入资产间网络化交互关系的可计算资产配置路径，以替代或补充传统Markowitz优化框架。

Result: 论文在五个股票市场、连续逆温度β和浓度γ的参数范围上进行了研究，并将选定的参数组合与长期多Markowitz前沿（long-only Markowitz frontiers）及标准基准进行比较。同一样本比较结果表明，该方法提供了一条从金融时间序列到网络调整后资产配置的可行计算路径；预测性交易表现不在研究范围内。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种从金融时间序列到网络化组合分配的可行且可计算的路线，在样本内与Markowitz前沿和标准基准具有可比性，但尚未涉及预测性交易性能的评估。

Abstract: We apply tensor-network methods to a field-coupled XY model for long-only portfolio construction. Daily return statistics set asset-specific fields and correlation couplings. Correlation distance, four-point Gromov hyperbolicity, and Ward clustering are used to construct a sparse interaction path. A Fourier-Bessel expansion maps the continuous angular partition function to a finite-current tensor network; bottom-up and top-down contractions then give the one-site marginals and equilibrium cosine scores. A softmax map converts these scores into positive, fully invested portfolio weights. We study five equity markets over continuous ranges of inverse temperature beta and concentration gamma, and compare selected parameter pairs with long-only Markowitz frontiers and standard benchmarks. The same-sample comparisons demonstrate a tractable route from financial time series to network-adjusted allocations; predictive trading performance is outside their scope.

</details>


### [90] [On the growth of operator entanglement in brickwork circuits with Yang--Baxter gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05121)
*Balázs Pozsgay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维砖墙（brickwork）电路中满足辫关系（braid relation）的双比特门（Yang–Baxter门）的算子纠缠（operator entanglement），建立了多类Yang–Baxter门算子Schmidt秩的均匀上界与多项式上界，并通过反例展示了无对偶幺正（dual unitarity）条件下算子Schmidt秩可呈指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子纠缠是刻画局部算子在量子电路演化中复杂度的核心量，而Yang–Baxter门代表一类具有特殊可积结构的两比特门。理解此类门在长时间演化下的算子纠缠增长规律，对于厘清可积系统中的纠缠动力学问边界，以及区分可积与混沌行为具有重要意义。

Method: 本文针对双比特门满足辫关系的一维砖墙电路展开理论分析，按结构对Yang–Baxter门进行分类，分别对一般qubit Yang–Baxter门、由非退化Yang–Baxter映射导出的置换门（permutation gate）、对合型对偶幺正Yang–Baxter门及其相位修饰（phase dressing）建立算子Schmidt秩（operator Schmidt rank）的上界估计；并通过构造显式反例来说明无对偶幺正约束时秩增长的指数行为。

Result: 研究确立了多类Yang–Baxter门的算子纠缠上界：所有qubit Yang–Baxter门及任意局域维数下由非退化Yang–Baxter映射导出的置换门，算子Schmidt秩均匀有界（对应常数的算子纠缠上界）；对合型对偶幺正Yang–Baxter门与任意相位修饰置换门的秩至多以多项式增长（对应对数纠缠上界）。同时构造了一个七态对合Yang–Baxter门及单比特算子，其精确算子Schmidt秩呈指数增长，而一般Yang–Baxter情形的纠缠增长仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: Yang–Baxter门在特定结构约束下可维持算子Schmidt秩的低增长，从而保证算子纠缠的常数或对数上界；但若缺乏对偶幺正等额外条件，指数增长的算子Schmidt秩在本研究中被证明可能发生。一般Yang–Baxter门情形下的纠缠增长规律仍是未解决的问题。所有证明与所选示例由ChatGPT 5.6 Sol构建。

Abstract: We study the operator entanglement of local operators in one-dimensional brickwork circuits whose two-site gate satisfies the braid relation; throughout this work, we call such a gate a Yang--Baxter gate. We establish upper bounds for several structured, overlapping classes of Yang--Baxter gates. We show that the operator Schmidt rank remains uniformly bounded in time for all qubit Yang--Baxter gates and, in arbitrary local dimension, for permutation gates obtained from non-degenerate Yang--Baxter maps. We also show that it grows at most polynomially for involutive dual-unitary Yang--Baxter gates and for arbitrary phase dressings of permutation gates obtained from non-degenerate Yang--Baxter maps. These results imply, respectively, constant and logarithmic upper bounds on the operator entanglement. Conversely, we construct a seven-state involutive Yang--Baxter gate without dual unitarity and a one-site operator whose exact operator Schmidt rank grows exponentially, although the corresponding operator entropies remain undetermined. Entanglement growth in the general Yang--Baxter case remains open. All proofs and selected examples were constructed by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol.

</details>


### [91] [Markov chains at the onset of non-reversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05183)
*Gustave Robichon,Cecile Monthus,Werner Krauth*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维路径图及其经站点复制构建的提升路径图（lifted path graph）上可逆马尔可夫链被扰动并逐渐转化为非可逆的机制，并通过格林矩阵（Green's matrix）形式体系计算多种稳态下的特征时间与加速效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解可逆马尔可夫链如何通过扰动逐步摆脱可逆性，以及非可逆性对特征值谱和动力学加速的影响，是统计力学和非平衡动力学中的基础问题。

Method: 作者在路径图及其提升路径图上，构造可逆随机游走作为起点，通过具体实例讨论非可逆转移矩阵的对角化与不可对角化情形，计算多种稳态（平坦型、方波型、楔形、V形）下的特征值谱，并发展出格林矩阵形式体系以用于计算Kemeny时间（Kemeny times）与平均首达时间（mean first-passage times）。

Result: 结果表明非可逆马尔可夫链的转移矩阵可能可对角化也可能不可对角化，特征值可为实数或复共轭对；借助特征值谱的比较揭示了提升（lifting）带来的动力学加速效应，格林矩阵形式体系有效给出特征时间并解释了不同稳态下的结果。

Conclusion: 格林矩阵形式体系为理解非可逆马尔可夫链的特征时间提供了统一框架，并通过特征值谱分析揭示了提升对加速扩散过程的作用机制。

Abstract: For a one-dimensional path graph and a lifted path graph constructed from a duplication of each of its sites, we study how a reversible Markov chain can be perturbed and gradually driven into non-reversibility. The reversible Markov chain has a transition matrix that is diagonalizable and features real-valued eigenvalues and eigenvectors. The left and right eigenvectors form a biorthogonal system. We discuss in concrete examples how the transition matrix of a non-reversible Markov chain may be diagonalizable or non-diagonalizable, and it may have real eigenvalues and complex-conjugate pairs. For a number of steady states (flat, square-wave, wedge, V-shape), we compute eigenvalue spectra on both graphs and discuss the speedup that can be achieved through lifting. We develop a Green's matrix formalism, which we use to compute Kemeny times and mean first-passage times, and which provides valuable information and allows us to interpret the results for the characteristic times.

</details>


### [92] [Non-reciprocally interacting Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes: Exceptional points, Anomalous relaxation, Pseudo-equilibrium and Boundary refrigeration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05391)
*Soumya Kanti Pal,Shamik Gupta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文建立了一类由可调非互易参数g控制的非互易相互作用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（NROU）模型层级结构。在特殊点g=g*处，漂移矩阵出现各种阶数的例外点（exceptional point），特征值和特征向量同时合并。层级涵盖非互易耦合二聚体、无序对应体系以及精确映射到Hatano-Nelson模型的多人体系链。研究发现三类关键现象：无序模型中例外点位置的普适边缘奇异性（edge singularity）；例外点处指数弛豫被多项式时间前因子修正；完全不对称多人体系中出现的“伪平衡”（pseudo-equilibrium）。此外，利用Harada-Sasa关系，得到了稳态总热耗散的闭合解析式，并揭示边界粒子随非互易度调谐从热源变为冷源的边界制冷效应（boundary refrigeration effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易相互作用广泛存在于活性系统、生物系统和无序系统中，并普遍使这些系统偏离平衡态。本文旨在建立一套可解析处理此类系统的层次化模型框架，从而在非互易参数与例外点物理之间建立清晰的对应关系。

Method: 作者引入由非互易参数g驱动的一系列非互易Ornstein-Uhlenbeck模型（NROU），通过设置g=g*使漂移矩阵非对角化，从而生成不同阶数的例外点。模型层级从非互易二聚体扩展到无序版本和多人体系链，后者精确映射到非厄米量子体系中的Hatano-Nelson模型。通过解析计算自相关函数和协方差函数、稳态分布及热耗散，并结合Harada-Sasa关系获得稳态总热耗散的闭合表达式。

Result: 无序模型的例外点位置g*在无序实现间的分布呈现普适边缘奇异性，恰好位于洁净体系的例外点处，并表现出明确的非自平均性质。在所有模型中，例外点处的指数弛豫被次数由例外点阶数决定的多项式时间前因子修正。在完全不对称极限下，多人体系链达到"伪平衡"，稳态分布可分解为平衡型的单粒子测度，同时存在非零稳态电流。N粒子相互作用系统分解为N/2个独立的复值OU过程。利用Harada-Sasa关系得到稳态总热耗散的闭合表达式，发现了边界制冷效应。

Conclusion: 非互易参数g的调控可将系统驱至例外点，产生普遍的低维奇异性、多项式弛豫修正和伪平衡等多层面物理现象。随非互易度连续调谐，边界粒子的角色从热源转变为冷源，揭示了非互易性（non-reciprocity）在驱动非平衡稳态和热力学行为中的核心作用。

Abstract: Non-reciprocal interactions are ubiquitous in active, biological, and disordered systems, generically driving them out of equilibrium. Here, we introduce a hierarchy of non-reciprocally interacting Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (NROU) models governed by a tunable non-reciprocity parameter $g$. At a special point $g=g^*$, the drift matrix becomes non-diagonalizable, realizing exceptional points (EP's) of different orders, where eigenvalues and eigenvectors simultaneously coalesce. The hierarchy encompasses non-reciprocally coupled dimers, their disordered counterparts, and a many-body chain exactly mapping onto the paradigmatic Hatano-Nelson model in the arena of non-Hermitian quantum systems. For the disordered model, we show that the distribution of the EP location $g^*$ across disorder realizations develops a universal edge singularity precisely at the clean-system EP, and is manifestly non-self-averaging. Across all models, we find that at the EP, the usual exponential relaxation of the autocorrelation and covariance functions is dressed by a polynomial-in-time prefactor whose degree is set by the order of the EP and whose detailed structure encodes the spatial architecture of the chain. At complete asymmetry, the many-body chain exhibits ``pseudo-equilibrium'': its steady-state distribution factorizes into equilibrium-like single-particle measures despite a nonzero steady-state current. Moreover, the $N$-particle interacting system decomposes into $N/2$ independent complex OU processes. Finally, using the Harada-Sasa relation, we obtain a closed-form expression for the total steady-state heat dissipation and uncover a boundary refrigeration effect, in which the boundary particles switch from acting as a hot to a cold reservoir as the non-reciprocity is tuned.

</details>

### [93] [Low-Dimensional Phase Diagram of Higher-Order Networked Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04692)
*Jia-Jie Qin,Jack Murdoch Moore,Xiaozhu Zhang,Gang Yan*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个解析性降维框架，将高阶网络动力学映射为有效低维系统，用于精确预测临界边界、双稳区域和相变性质，揭示了高阶相互作用对转变连续性和滞回性的不同影响，并发现了系统韧性与二体、高阶连接的一致性之间的深刻关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶网络展现出超越传统二体（pairwise）模型所能描述的丰富临界现象，需要一个统一的分析框架来理解其临界转变。

Method: 开发了一个解析性的维度约减（dimension-reduction）框架，将高阶网络动力学映射到一个有效的低维系统上，从而在解析层面捕捉系统的临界行为。

Result: 该框架在多种动力学过程中均展现出预测能力，揭示了高阶相互作用对转变连续性与滞回现象的独特效应，并发现二体与高阶连接的对齐程度强烈影响系统韧性，其中同配混合（assortative mixing）会增强向活跃态的倾覆。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解高阶网络中的临界转变建立了普适理论，为预判和管理复杂系统中的系统性风险提供了新的洞见。

Abstract: Higher-order networks exhibit rich critical phenomena that cannot be captured by traditional pairwise models. Here, we develop an analytical dimension-reduction framework that maps higher-order networked dynamics onto an effective low-dimensional system, allowing accurate prediction of tipping boundaries, bistability regions, and the nature of phase transitions. We demonstrate the power of this framework across a range of dynamical processes, revealing distinct effects of higher-order interactions on transition continuity and hysteresis. Furthermore, we find that system resilience exhibits a profound dependence on the alignment between pairwise and higher-order connectivity, with assortative mixing enhancing tipping toward active states. Our findings establish a general theory for understanding the critical transitions in higher-order networks, offering new insights for anticipating and managing systemic risk in complex systems.

</details>

### [94] [Eye-opening bounds on cusps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04302)
*Ryan A. Lanzetta,Ian Moult,Yifan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从保形场论中线缺陷的尖角反常维度出发，利用反射正性、定域性和共形不变性，推导出新的非微扰不等式——"共形凹性"，并由此建立了线缺陷融合规则与局部算符数据之间的定量联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于尖角反常维度（cusp anomalous dimension）的角度凹性（angular concavity）约束较弱，作者希望寻找更严格的非微扰限制，并探索尖角几何中光滑极限与融合极限之间的深层关联。

Method: 对一对构成"眼睛"几何的尖角施加反射正性（reflection positivity）、定域性（locality）和共形不变性（conformal invariance），推导出尖角反常维度需满足的新条件，作者称之为共形凹性（conformal concavity）。该推导同样适用于涉及不同线缺陷的尖角情形。

Result: 新导出的共形凹性约束远强于已知的角度凹性，且能直接联系光滑极限与融合极限，进一步给出缺陷改变算符维数、Casimir能量以及次子融合数据的边界。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了保形场论（conformal field theory）中局部算符数据与线算符融合规则之间一种新的定量桥梁，为理解线缺陷的动力学结构提供了更深刻的非微扰约束。

Abstract: We derive new nonperturbative inequalities on the cusp anomalous dimensions of conformal line defects. We impose reflection positivity, locality, and conformal invariance on a pair of cusps forming an ``eye'' geometry, deriving a novel condition on the cusp anomalous dimension which we refer to as conformal concavity. The resulting constraint is much stronger than the known angular concavity, and our derivation applies also to cusps involving distinct line defects. Remarkably, it directly links the smooth and fusion limits, yielding bounds on defect-changing operator dimensions, Casimir energies, and subleading fusion data. We thus uncover a new quantitative bridge between aspects of the local operator data and the fusion rules of line operators in conformal field theories.

</details>


### [95] [Boundary-Boundary Duality on Regular Black Holes, Supersymmetric Solitons and Holographic Spinning Plasma Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04331)
*Andres Anabalon,Horatiu Nastase*

Main category: hep-th

Abstract: We construct a new family of exact rotating solutions of four-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell theory with negative cosmological constant describing regular spinning AdS black holes and smooth solitons. The geometries are free of curvature singularities and admit open regions of parameter space without closed timelike curves, while supersymmetric limits correspond to causally regular horizonless configurations. A remarkable feature of these solutions is the existence of at least two codimension-one conformal boundaries. The first is Minkowski spacetime, where the expectation value of the energy--momentum tensor describes a finite spinning disk of strongly coupled conformal plasma whose edge rotates at the speed of light. The second boundary is a rotating black-hole geometry with vanishing energy--momentum tensor but non-vanishing Cotton tensor, defining a holographic theory in which the dual graviton is fixed at the boundary. We show that the energy density associated with the dual graviton exactly reproduces that of the plasma after a suitable analytic continuation and the identification of the holographic energy scale with the Lorentz factor of the rotating fluid, providing evidence for a boundary-boundary duality. Indeed, electromagnetic duality exchanges the electric and magnetic currents between the boundary supporting the graviton and that supporting the dual graviton thus providing a generalized mirror symmetry between these boundary theories. Hence, the Lorentz factor is identified with the electromagnetic dual of the renormalization scale. In an appropriate limit, the solutions reduce to the planar Reissner--Nordström--AdS black hole, whereas the supersymmetric solitons have no regular static limit.

Result: 在Q超选择下，仅n=0扇区具有非零范数（nonvanishing norms）；物理态条件将态投影到此扇区；对于MM*对，R-本征态为纠缠对（entangled pairs），R超选择规则确保了正概率（positive probabilities）。文中进一步讨论了光学定理（optical theorem）如何描述穿越这些纠缠对的物理割线（physical cuts）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过Q和R两类超选择规则，带有复共轭极点的理论可以在保持正概率的前提下获得一致的物理诠释，且纠缠对结构为理解光学定理中的物理割线提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The superselection-rule approach to ghost theories is extended to theories with a nonreal spectrum containing a complex-conjugate pair of poles on the physical sheet. The corresponding generalized ghost parity $Q$ assigns a phase $e^{inα}$ to each sector. For a $MM^*$ pair carrying real total energy and momentum, we define a swap operation $R$. The $R$-eigenstates are entangled pairs, and an $R$ superselection rule ensures positive probabilities. Abstract: The problem of the effective excitations of a gauge theory in the nonperturbative regime remains not completely understood. Abstract: Open-closed-open triality relates two distinct open string descriptions through a common closed string theory. Starting from determinant insertions representing giant gravitons, we formulate a V-type open-string Gaussian three-matrix model and, through a color-flavor transformation, derive an F-type open-string model of three bifundamental matrices forming a triangular quiver. Its closed quiver walks include oriented cubic cycles, a feature absent from the bipartite two-point case. We test the correspondence by deriving exact finite-$N$ single-giant polynomials, recovering the known F-type large-$N$ saddles, and proving the equivalence of the V- and F-type character expansions via an explicit bijection of their combinatorial data. We argue that the corresponding target-space construction is naturally described by a barycentrically subdivided Belyi map or a colored Hurwitz constellation.

Abstract: We study how finite-$N$ invariant theory organizes the non-perturbative structure of matrix models. For a model of four traceless Hermitian $2\times 2$ matrices, the Hironaka decomposition realizes the gauge-invariant configuration space as an eight-sheeted branched cover of the space of primary invariants. We show that ramification points of this cover naturally generate additional saddle points when the action depends only on the primaries. For an explicit rank-two saddle, we compute its action, one-loop normalization and Picard--Lefschetz connection to the perturbative vacuum. The same saddle controls the leading Borel singularity and large-order growth of perturbation theory, while its first fluctuation correction reproduces the first subleading large-order correction with no fitted parameters. Abstract: We develop a cluster-algebraic description of symbol alphabets for chiral stress-tensor form factors in planar $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang--Mills theory. At four points, the folding leads to the $\widetilde{\mathrm{VI}}$ cluster algebra, which has infinitely many cluster variables. Tropical truncation selects a finite set of rational coordinates and four physical limit rays. The $A_1^{(1)}$ mutation sequences approaching these rays generate the four square roots and the corresponding algebraic-letter spaces. Within the resulting finite set of rational candidates, antipodal closure excludes precisely eight additional letters. On the parity-preserving surface, the antipodal map is realized, after a further folding, by two commuting mutations followed by a relabelling. Furthermore, we find an alternative quiver within the same mutation class that becomes self-antipodal after parity folding, mirroring the antipodal self-duality of the four-point MHV form factor. Its double- and triple-collinear boundaries reproduce, respectively, the $C_2$ algebra of the three-point form factor and the $A_3$ algebra of the six-point amplitude; after folding the latter to $C_2$, the antipodal map exchanges the two $C_2$ boundaries.

</details>


### [103] [What is Classical Null String Theory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04844)
*M. M. Sheikh-Jabbari,H. Yavartanoo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视D维闵可夫斯基靶空间中零弦（null string）的传统定义，区分了部分规范化的卡罗尔西格玛模型（Carrollian sigma-model）与真正的零弦理论。作者论证ILST作用量仅规范了世界片微分同胚和共同外尔（Weyl）标度，却未规范卡罗尔-外尔（Carroll-Weyl）标度，进而指出零弦理论必须补充这一对称性的规范，并发现该规范在有限张力（finite tension）下受阻，在零张力下则产生附加的一阶约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的零弦定义存在模糊性：ILST作用量只部分化了规范对称性，尚未构成物理上自洽的弦理论。作者旨在明确从部分规范化的卡罗尔西格玛模型到完全规范的零弦理论所需的补充条件。

Method: 作者对ILST作用量进行对称性分析，识别其仅规范了世界片微分同胚与共同外尔标度而遗漏了卡罗尔-外尔标度。通过逐例考察该标度的规范可行性，在有限张力与零张力两种情形下分别展开约束分析，并利用零世界片作为零测地线汇（null geodesic congruence）的图像给出靶空间诠释。

Result: 在最小实现中，卡罗尔-外尔标度的规范在有限张力下受阻；在零张力下则产生附加的一阶（first-class）约束，并由此确立沿规范轨线的物理构型识别。零世界片的零测地线汇描述为规范卡罗尔-外尔标度提供了互补的靶空间解释。

Conclusion: 作者由此给出了将ILST理论转化为完整零弦理论所需的补充要求，明确了物理弦理论要求所有局部世界片代表变换均被规范化的判据。

Abstract: We revisit the classical definition of a null string in a D-dimensional Minkowski target space and distinguish a consistent partially-gauged Carrollian sigma-model from the null string theory. The Isberg-Lindström-Sundborg-Theodoridis (ILST) action gauges worldsheet diffeomorphisms and the common Weyl rescaling, but not the independent relative rescaling of the temporal and spatial representatives of the intrinsic Carrollian structure, Carroll-Weyl scaling. A physical string theory requires every local change of worldsheet representative to be gauged, therefore, the Carroll-Weyl symmetry should be gauged in a null string theory. In the minimal realization, the corresponding gauging is obstructed at finite tension, whereas at zero tension it yields an additional first-class constraint and the associated identification of physical configurations along its gauge orbits. The description of the null worldsheet as a congruence of null geodesics provides a complementary target-space interpretation of gauging of the Carroll-Weyl scaling. Our analysis thus provides the supplementary requirements that turns the ILST theory into the null string theory.

</details>


### [104] [de Sitter and S-folds in type IIB from $\mathcal{N} = 4$ $D = 4$ gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05004)
*Colin Sterckx*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对具有de Sitter（dS₄）解的N=4、D=4纯SO(4)-规范超引力（gauged supergravity）的type IIB提升（uplift）进行分类，并通过对内部空间沿非紧致方向进行S-折叠（S-fold）商化，构造了一个有限且非零四维有效牛顿常数的经典type IIB de Sitter解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maldacena-Nunez不可能性定理（no-go theorem）表明在type IIB超引力中经典de Sitter解的构造面临障碍，因此需要寻找新的途径来规避该定理的限制。

Method: 将dS₄解的type IIB提升分类为黎曼曲面（Riemann surface）Σ上两个S²纤维化（fibration）的内部几何，由Σ上的一对调和函数（harmonic functions）h₁,₂刻画；随后在Σ = ℝ × I情形下对非紧致方向进行S-折叠商化。

Result: 分类给出了type IIB中de Sitter解的一个新族（new family）；通过S-折叠商化成功规避了Maldacena-Nunez不可能性定理的假设，获得了一个经典type IIB超引力的de Sitter解，且其四维有效牛顿常数（effective Newton constant）为有限且非零。

Conclusion: 该方法为在弦理论框架中构造经典de Sitter真空提供了一条可行的技术路线，并扩展了具有AdS解的超引力提升结果到de Sitter情形。

Abstract: In this paper, we classify the type IIB uplifts of the $\mathcal{N}=4$ $D=4$ pure $\text{SO}(4)$-gauged supergravity admitting a dS$_4$ solution. As for their AdS cousins, the internal geometries of these uplifts consist of two $S^2$ fibred over a Riemann surface $Σ$, and are classified by pairs of harmonic functions $h_{1,\,2}$ on $Σ$, providing a new family of de Sitter solutions in type IIB. Choosing specific functions $h_{1,\,2}$ on $Σ= \mathbb{R} \times I$, we perform an S-fold quotient of the internal space along its non-compact direction. This S-folding allows us to evade the assumptions of the Maldacena-Nunez no-go theorem and provides a classical de Sitter solution of type IIB supergravity with finite and non-vanishing four-dimensional effective Newton constant.

</details>


### [105] [The endless journey towards the horizon of a quantum black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05164)
*Victor Franken,Thomas G. Mertens,Bruno de S. L. Torres*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文表明，由于黑洞谱满足随机矩阵统计所体现的强混沌性，适当引力穿着的下落观测者需要无限本征时间（proper time）才能到达视界，从而使黑洞内部在操作上对外部观测者不可达，标志着量子引力中黑洞大N因果结构的锐利断裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力的核心问题之一是量子效应是否在黑洞视界附近产生非平庸结构，以及黑洞内部是否是大量N极限的涌现特征。作者希望检验这一基本问题，探索黑洞内部的物理可达性与大N极限描述之间的关系。

Method: 作者通过二维量子黑洞背景中下落粒子探测器与物质场耦合的响应建模下落观测者的体验，并结合黑洞能谱的随机矩阵统计来刻画其强混沌性质。

Result: 研究发现，引力穿着下落观测者需要无限本征时间才能到达黑洞视界，使得黑洞内部对外部观测者在操作上不可达。粒子探测器在进入靠近视界的无限长非微扰旅程时，其激发概率出现峰值。

Conclusion: 黑洞的强混沌性导致其内部在量子引力层面操作上不可达，此前基于大N极限预测的黑洞因果结构在量子效应下发生断裂，黑洞视界附近存在由量子效应驱动的非平庸结构。

Abstract: It is a problem of great importance in quantum gravity whether quantum effects lead to nontrivial structure near black hole horizons, and if their interiors are emergent features of a large $N$ limit. We show that the strongly chaotic nature of black holes, captured by random matrix statistics of their spectrum, makes it so that a suitably gravitationally dressed infalling observer takes an infinite amount of proper time to reach the horizon. This makes the black hole interior operationally inaccessible to any outside observer, marking a sharp breakdown of the large $N$ causal structure of black holes in quantum gravity. To model the experience of an infalling observer, we study the response of an infalling particle detector coupled to a matter field in a 2d quantum black hole background, finding a peak in the detector's excitation probability as it enters an infinitely long non-perturbative journey close to the black hole horizon.

</details>


### [106] [CFT characters from localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05211)
*Lorenz Eberhardt,Duarte Fragoso,Hessel Posthuma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从共伴随轨道（coadjoint orbit）几何上计算二维（超）共形场论（(S)CFT）手征代数（chiral algebra）特征标（character）的一般性方法，类比于有限维李群的共伴随轨道方法，并展示了该方法可简洁地重新推导Virasoro代数、Kac-Moody代数及其超对称推广的特征标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维（超）共形场论中手征代数的特征标计算是重要而复杂的任务，作者希望建立一种更为一般且几何直观的计算框架，类比有限维李群中成熟的共伴随轨道方法（coadjoint orbit method），为处理不同类型的手征代数提供统一路径。

Method: 作者提出一种通用的几何程序，通过共伴随轨道来构造并计算二维（超）共形场论中手征代数的特征标。该方法在结构上类比有限维李群中的共伴随轨道方法，将代数特征标的计算转化为几何对象上的构造。

Result: 该几何方法被成功应用于Virasoro代数、Kac-Moody代数及其超对称推广，给出这些代数特征标的简洁重新推导（rederivation），验证了方法的有效性和普遍适用性。

Conclusion: 共伴随轨道方法可以被推广到无穷维手征代数（chiral algebra）的特征标计算中，为二维（超）共形场论中多种代数结构的统一几何处理提供了新途径。

Abstract: We explain a general procedure to compute characters of chiral algebras of 2d (S)CFTs geometrically from their coadjoint orbits, in analogy with the coadjoint orbit method for finite-dimensional Lie groups. We demonstrate that this method can be used to give concise rederivations of the characters of the Virasoro algebra, Kac-Moody algebras, as well as supersymmetric generalizations.

</details>


### [107] [Infrared Memory and Scrambling in a Dynamically Opened Coupled-SYK Majorana Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05336)
*Ali Vahedi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究由两个耦合Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）系统构成的动态开启Majorana结，通过时间相关的双线性耦合驱动系统从近解耦到强杂化区域，发现开启过程保留强红外记忆，并用精确模拟验证了增强的扇区间纠缠和交叉扇区算符加扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在SYK系统中，动态开启Majorana结是否能在超越二次极限之外仍保留长波记忆，以及在正规序（conformal）红外结构与有限尺寸多体加扰之间建立联系，是尚未解决的问题。

Method: 采用大N（large-N）Keldysh理论在共形q=4 SYK鞍点附近的低频分析，以及对两个N=8 SYK团簇进行精确有限维数值模拟。

Result: 低频分析发现开启过程保留强红外记忆，关联的异常响应对红外截断日益敏感；N=8精确模拟显示开启后扇区间纠缠增强、宇称转移和交叉扇区算符加扰效应出现。

Conclusion: 结果表明共形SYK动力学的红外结构与有限尺寸多体加扰存在内在联系，动态开启的相互作用Majorana系统能够在超越二次极限的情况下保留长波记忆。

Abstract: We study a dynamically opened Majorana junction formed by two coupled Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) systems. A time-dependent bilinear coupling drives the system from a nearly decoupled to a strongly hybridized regime, providing an interacting realization of a Majorana shutter. Using a low-frequency analysis of the large-$N$ Keldysh theory around the conformal $q=4$ SYK saddle, we find that the opening retains a strong infrared memory, with the associated anomalous response becoming increasingly sensitive to the infrared cutoff. Exact finite-dimensional simulations of two $N=8$ SYK clusters show enhanced inter-sector entanglement, parity transfer, and cross-sector operator scrambling after the opening. These results connect the infrared structure of conformal SYK dynamics with finite-size many-body scrambling and show how a dynamically opened interacting Majorana system can retain long-wavelength memory beyond the quadratic limit.

</details>


### [108] [Decompactification and the Flat-Space Limit of AdS$_5\times$ S$^5$ Master Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05409)
*Burkhard Eden,Paul Heslop,Harshal Kulkarni,Arthur Lipstein,Romain Ruzziconi,Akshay Yelleshpur Srikant*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究AdS5/CFT4全息关联函数在同时考虑内禀S^5退紧致化条件下的平坦时空极限，提出用主传播子和主关联子来描述这一极限，并论证其可分别恢复5维和10维平坦时空中的散射振幅，进而讨论平坦时空全息性与共形场论Carroll极限的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS/CFT对偶在平坦时空极限（flat-space limit）下的行为是全息原理拓展的重要问题，而此前研究多忽略内禀球面的紧致化效应。本文旨在建立一种自然框架，同时处理AdS空间的退紧致化与内部S^5的解紧致化，以获得完整的平坦时空散射振幅。

Method: 作者引入主传播子（master propagator）和主关联子（master correlator）的概念，通过重求和无穷多Kaluza–Klein模式来刻画平坦时空极限。对边界关联子，研究其运动学限制到5维的振幅结构；对保持完整AdS5×S^5体空间的关联子则分析其恢复10维无约束运动学。此外，通过解析延拓球面至dS5使几何成为AdS5×dS5，研究平坦极限与边界极限的可交换性。

Result: 研究表明，在同时考虑S^5退紧致化时，边界关联子在平坦时空极限下可表示为运动学限制于5维的振幅；若极限前外源插入保持在5维反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）时空×5维球（S^5）体空间中，则恢复10维闵可夫斯基（Minkowski）空间无限制运动学的振幅。进一步解析延拓球面后，获得具有两个时间方向的平坦时空振幅，其10维运动学满足两个同时的零约束条件（null constraints）。

Conclusion: 本文的工作为平坦时空全息性（flat-space holography）提供了新的技术手段，并揭示了其与对偶共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）的Carroll极限之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We study the flat-space limit of AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$ holographic correlators while accounting for the simultaneous decompactification of the internal S$^5$. We show that this limit is naturally formulated in terms of master propagators and correlators, which resum the infinite tower of Kaluza--Klein modes. For boundary correlators, the resulting expressions can be interpreted in terms of amplitudes with kinematics restricted to 5d. We then consider master correlators whose external insertions are kept in the AdS$_5\times$S$^5$ bulk before taking the limit, and argue that they recover flat-space amplitudes with unrestricted kinematics in 10d Minkowski space. Finally, we show that the flat-space limit commutes with the boundary limit if we analytically continue the sphere so that the bulk geometry becomes AdS$_5 \times$dS$_5$, and the resulting formulae suggest an interpretation in terms of amplitudes in flat space with two time directions and with 10d kinematics satisfying two simultaneous null constraints. We discuss the implications of these constructions for flat-space holography and its relation to the Carrollian limit of the dual CFT.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>
