### [159] [Enhanced Multifractality Induced by Non-Hermitian Disorder in Quantum Percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04625)
*W. S. Oliveira,Julian Faundez,Rodrigo Arouca,Welles Morgado*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究二维量子位置渗透模型中几何稀释与非厄米无序的相互作用，通过引入随机的虚部局域势（模拟非空间相关的增益与损耗），并利用复能级间距统计、参与熵与多重分形分析，发现非厄米无序会将量子渗透阈值推向更大的占据概率，完全离域相被逐渐抑制乃至消失，但中间区域仍呈现真正的多重分形临界相，且量子渗透转变的标度指数保持了厄米情形下的普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者旨在探究非厄米（non-Hermitian）无序——具体为随机虚部势能所代表的非空间相关增益与损耗——如何与几何稀释在二维量子渗透模型中相互作用，影响本征态的局域化性质及渗透转变的临界行为。

Method: 通过在保持跳迁振幅互易的前提下，将随机虚部局域势引入量子位置渗透格点，随后结合复杂能级间距统计、参与熵与多重分形分析三种工具表征本征态局域化性质；并采用有限尺寸标度来分析无序强度与非厄米强度对渗透阈值和临界指数的影响，并与等强度的实部（厄米）局域势进行比对。

Result: 有限尺寸标度结果显示，非厄米无序使量子渗透阈值向更大占据概率方向移动；完全离域相被逐渐压制，并在足够强的无序下消失。该效应并非简单地等于同强度实部无场所导致的阈值漂移，而是由虚部特性放大——等强度的实部（厄米）无序产生的阈值漂移较弱。然而经典渗透阈值与量子渗透阈值之间的中间区域表现为真正的多重分形临界相，且局域化长度指数保留其厄米值。

Conclusion: 随机的增益与损耗增强多重分形临界区域，同时保持了量子渗透转变的普适类，说明非厄米无序在本体系中不改变转变的普适性，只放大临界区间的存在范围。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay between geometric dilution and non-Hermitian disorder in the two-dimensional quantum site-percolation model. Non-Hermiticity is introduced through random imaginary on-site potentials, representing spatially uncorrelated gain and loss, while the hopping amplitudes remain reciprocal. By combining complex level-spacing statistics, participation entropy, and multifractal analysis, we characterize the localization properties of the eigenstates as functions of the disorder and the non-Hermiticity strength. Our finite-size scaling results show that non-Hermitian disorder shifts the quantum percolation threshold ($p_q$) toward larger occupation probabilities. Consequently, the fully delocalized phase is progressively suppressed and disappears at sufficiently strong disorder. This suppression is not a simple consequence of adding on-site disorder of a given strength, but is specifically enhanced by its imaginary character, as an equally strong real (Hermitian) on-site potential produces a weaker shift of $p_q$. Nevertheless, the intermediate region between the classical ($p_c$) and quantum percolation thresholds presents a genuine multifractal critical phase, while the localization-length exponent $ν$ remains the same, relative to its Hermitian value. Altogether, our results demonstrate that random gain and loss enhance the multifractal regime while preserving the universality class of the quantum percolation transition.

</details>


### [160] [Connecting heterogeneous dynamics with local entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05276)
*Jun Wu,Walter Kob,Yujie Wang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种加权对熵描述符（weighted pair-entropy descriptor），通过在传统的两体超额熵（two-body excess entropy）基础上乘以与对关联函数（pair correlation function）长度尺度相关的权重函数，将中程有序（medium-range order）对结构的贡献纳入考量，从而显著提升了玻璃体系中静态结构与弛豫动力学之间的关联预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立静态结构与异质弛豫动力学之间稳健且具有物理可解释性的联系是玻璃物理（glass physics）中的根本性挑战。传统熵类描述符仅关注短程有序，未能充分捕捉中程距离上的结构信息，导致其预测能力有限。

Method: 在计算传统两体超额熵的积分式中，引入一个与对关联函数长度尺度直接相关的权重函数作为乘子。该操作不影响短程有序对局部超额熵的贡献，但能够将中间距离上（即中程有序）的结构信息纳入描述符中，从而构建加权的对熵描述符。

Result: 针对典型的二维Lennard-Jones玻璃形成体（glass former），该描述符在长时间的（α弛豫时间的倍数）粒级动力学倾向（dynamical propensity）上表现出强相关性，最大结构-动力学相关系数约为0.9，显著优于传统局部对超额熵的预测能力。

Conclusion: 将具有物理动机的结构长度尺度纳入熵类描述符，能够在保持物理可解释性的同时大幅提升其预测能力。该工作为研究玻璃形成体系中结构-动力学关联提供了一种简单且通用的框架。

Abstract: Establishing a robust and physically interpretable link between static structure and heterogeneous relaxation dynamics remains a fundamental challenge in glass physics. Here, we introduce a weighted pair-entropy descriptor based on the conventional two-body excess entropy. For this, we multiply the integrand used to calculate the excess entropy by a weight function that is directly related to the length scale of the pair correlation function. This multiplication does not affect the contribution of the short range order to the local excess entropy, but allows to take into account the structure present on intermediate distances, i.e., the medium-range order. For a canonical two-dimensional Lennard-Jones glass former, the resulting descriptor exhibits a strong correlation with particle-level dynamical propensity at long times (multiples of the alpha-relaxation time), with a maximum structure--dynamics correlation reaching about 0.9, substantially outperforming the predictive power of the conventional local pair excess entropy. These results demonstrate that incorporating a physically motivated structural length scale into entropy-based descriptors markedly enhances their predictive power while preserving physical interpretability. Our findings provide a simple and general framework for investigating structure--dynamics correlations in glass-forming systems.

</details>

### [161] [q-Opers and Quantum/Classical Duality Beyond Type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04739)
*Peter Koroteev,Myungbo Shim,Rahul Singh*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用 q-oper 的语言，提出了一种代数几何描述，用以阐明量子 XXZ 自旋链的 Bethe Ansatz 方程与经典群上的多体三角 Ruijsenaars-Schneider/Macdonald 系统之间的对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将先前仅适用于 GL(N) XXZ 自旋链与 N 体 Ruijsenaars-Schneider 系统（带扭曲周期边界条件）的对偶结果推广到经典群（B/C/D 型）情形，从而建立更广泛的对应字典。

Method: 作者在 (GL(N), q)-oper 数据和 N 体三角 Ruijsenaars-Schneider 模型两个层面实施 Z/2Z 折叠（folding）操作，给出非约化 BC 型系统的描述，并利用代数几何框架建立经典哈密顿量的能量级集合与 XXZ Bethe Ansatz 方程解集之间的双射。

Result: 在该对偶关系下，经典 B/C/D 型哈密顿系统的能量级集与 A 型 XXZ Bethe Ansatz 方程（带开放边界条件）的解集一一对应；此外，非约化（nonreduced）BC 型系统也出现在分析中。

Conclusion: 该结果将贝特拟设方程与可积经典系统之间的对偶从 GL(N) 情形推广至经典群，为理解 XXZ 自旋链与 Ruijsenaars-Schneider/Macdonald 系统在不同边界条件下的深层联系提供了统一的代数几何框架。

Abstract: Using the language of q-opers, we propose an algebro-geometric description of the duality between Bethe Ansatz equations for quantum XXZ spin chains and many-body trigonometric Ruijsenaars-Schneider/Macdonald systems for classical groups. Upon this duality, the energy level sets of the classical B/C/D-type Hamiltonians are found to be in bijection with the set of solutions of the XXZ Bethe Ansatz equations of type A, albeit with open boundary conditions. This dictionary generalizes previous results in which a GL(N) XXZ spin chain with twisted periodic boundary conditions was dual to an N-body Ruijsenaars-Schneider system. Our construction implements a $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ folding both at the level of the (GL(N),q)-oper data as well as at the level of the N-body trigonometric Ruijsenaars-Schneider model. The nonreduced BC-type systems appear in our analysis as well.

</details>
